   52 +// QSGSketch records the universal/analytical proof obligations. No Verif
       y.
   53 +type QSGSketch struct {
   54 +    Steps []SketchStep
   55 +}
   56 +
   57 +// BuildSketch returns the six unsupported obligations of (T).
   58 +func BuildSketch() QSGSketch {
   59 +    return QSGSketch{Steps: []SketchStep{
   60 +        {ID: "SK-1", Statement: "universal conservation identity (T) over
        all Hadamard states and backgrounds", Evidence: "doc 04 (2.3); human pro
       of", Status: StatusSketchUnsupported},
   61 +        {ID: "SK-2", Statement: "Synge-rule divergence computation (D1) w
       ith transport identity (TI)", Evidence: "doc 04 §1.3–§1.4; human proof",
       Status: StatusSketchUnsupported},
   62 +        {ID: "SK-3", Statement: "Hadamard recursion closure and remainder
        limit (1.8)", Evidence: "doc 03 §1; human proof", Status: StatusSketchUn
       supported},
   63 +        {ID: "SK-4", Statement: "point-splitting subtraction procedure an
       d coincidence limits", Evidence: "doc 03 §§0–2; human procedure", Status:
        StatusSketchUnsupported},
   64 +        {ID: "SK-5", Statement: "Hadamard state semantics and two-point f
       unction properties", Evidence: "doc 03 P1/P2; human semantics", Status: S
       tatusSketchUnsupported},
   65 +        {ID: "SK-6", Statement: "bi-differential operator algebra and par
       allel transport", Evidence: "doc 03 §0.4, doc 04 §1.2; human proof", Stat
       us: StatusSketchUnsupported},
   66 +    }}
   67 +}
   68 +
   69 +// Fixture holds the store plus every pinned reference. All gates in Buil
       d
   70 +// and Verify compare resolved payload bytes, never bare references.
   71 +type Fixture struct {
   72 +    Store physmath.Store
   73 +
   74 +    DCombo6, DCancel, DHalf, DTrace, DFlat artifact.ArtifactRef
   75 +
   76 +    ComboResult, ComboExpected   artifact.ArtifactRef
   77 +    CancelResult, CancelExpected artifact.ArtifactRef
   78 +    HalfResult, HalfExpected     artifact.ArtifactRef
   79 +    TraceResult, TraceExpected   artifact.ArtifactRef
   80 +    FlatResult, FlatExpected     artifact.ArtifactRef
   81 +    NonzeroRef, ZeroRef          artifact.ArtifactRef
   82 +
   83 +    V0Shape, V0AntiB   artifact.ArtifactRef
   84 +    H1Shape, H1AntiB   artifact.ArtifactRef
   85 +    H2Shape, H2AntiB   artifact.ArtifactRef
   86 +    FLRW001            artifact.ArtifactRef
   87 +
   88 +    Sketch QSGSketch
   89 +}
   90 +
   91 +func resolvePayload(store physmath.Store, ref artifact.ArtifactRef) ([]by
       te, error) {
   92 +    _, payload, err := store.Lookup(ref)
   93 +    return payload, err
   94 +}
   95 +
   96 +func applyRule(store physmath.Store, rule string, inputs ...artifact.Arti
       factRef) (artifact.ArtifactRef, error) {
   97 +    res, err := physmath.Apply(store, physmath.Invocation{
   98 +        ProducerNamespace: "physmath",
   99 +        RuleID:            rule,
  100 +        InputRefs:         inputs,
  101 +    })
  102 +    if err != nil {
  103 +        return artifact.ArtifactRef{}, err
  104 +    }
  105 +    return res.OutputRef, nil
  106 +}
  107 +
  108 +func mintDerivation(store physmath.Store, premises []artifact.ArtifactRef
       , steps []physmath.DerivationStep, results []artifact.ArtifactRef) (artif
       act.ArtifactRef, error) {
  109 +    drv := physmath.Derivation{PremiseRefs: premises, Steps: steps, Resul
       tRefs: results}
  110 +    if err := drv.Validate(); err != nil {
  111 +        return artifact.ArtifactRef{}, err
  112 +    }
  113 +    raw, err := drv.Encode()
  114 +    if err != nil {
  115 +        return artifact.ArtifactRef{}, err
  116 +    }
  117 +    return store.Put(physmath.FamilyDerivation, raw)
  118 +}
  119 +
  120 +func sym(display string, ordinal uint32) physmath.Expr {
  121 +    return physmath.NewBoundSymbol(display, physmath.DeriveParameterID(or
       dinal))
  122 +}
  123 +
  124 +func rat(num, den int64) physmath.Expr { return physmath.MustRational(num
       , den) }
  125 +func neg(e physmath.Expr) physmath.Expr { return physmath.Neg(e) }
  126 +
  127 +// arithIdentity mints premise input, simplifies it, gates the output
  128 +// against expected, and packages the single-step derivation.
  129 +func arithIdentity(store physmath.Store, input, expected physmath.Expr, t
       ag string) (in, out, exp, drv artifact.ArtifactRef, err error) {
  130 +    if in, err = physmath.PutExpression(store, input); err != nil {
  131 +        return in, out, exp, drv, fmt.Errorf("%s put input: %w", tag, err
       )
  132 +    }
  133 +    out, err = applyRule(store, physmath.RuleExprSimplify, in)
  134 +    if err != nil {
  135 +        return in, out, exp, drv, fmt.Errorf("%s simplify: %w", tag, err)
  136 +    }
  137 +    if exp, err = physmath.PutExpression(store, expected); err != nil {
  138 +        return in, out, exp, drv, fmt.Errorf("%s put expected: %w", tag,
       err)
  139 +    }
  140 +    rawOut, err := resolvePayload(store, out)
  141 +    if err != nil {
  142 +        return in, out, exp, drv, fmt.Errorf("%s resolve out: %w", tag, e
       rr)
  143 +    }
  144 +    rawExp, err := resolvePayload(store, exp)
  145 +    if err != nil {
  146 +        return in, out, exp, drv, fmt.Errorf("%s resolve exp: %w", tag, e
       rr)
  147 +    }
  148 +    if !bytes.Equal(rawOut, rawExp) {
  149 +        return in, out, exp, drv, fmt.Errorf("%s gate: output bytes diffe
       r", tag)
  150 +    }
  151 +    drv, err = mintDerivation(store, []artifact.ArtifactRef{in}, []physma
       th.DerivationStep{
  152 +        {Ordinal: 0, ProducerNamespace: "physmath", RuleID: physmath.Rule
       ExprSimplify, InputRefs: []artifact.ArtifactRef{in}, OutputRef: out},
  153 +    }, []artifact.ArtifactRef{out})
  154 +    if err != nil {
  155 +        return in, out, exp, drv, fmt.Errorf("%s derivation: %w", tag, er
       r)
  156 +    }
  157 +    return in, out, exp, drv, nil
  158 +}
  159 +
  160 +// Build mints every golden, derivation, and the sketch. Hand-derivation
  161 +// sources: doc 04 (1.7) for COMBO-6, (1.3)+(2.1) for CANCEL, (D1) for
  162 +// HALF, (3.4) for TRACE, (1.9) for FLAT-CHAIN and NONZERO, (V0)/(3.1)/
  163 +// (3.2) for shapes, doc 05 G4 for FLRW-001.
  164 +func Build() (*Fixture, error) {
  165 +    store := physmath.NewMemStore()
  166 +    fx := &Fixture{Store: store}
  167 +    put := func(e physmath.Expr) (artifact.ArtifactRef, error) {
  168 +        return physmath.PutExpression(store, e)
  169 +    }
  170 +
  171 +    // COMBO-6: 2+4=6 — the (1.7) decomposition (2V1/sigma part + 4V1
  172 +    // transport part). Rational skeleton only; the analysis is SK-3.
  173 +    _, comboOut, comboExp, comboDrv, err := arithIdentity(store,
  174 +        physmath.Add(rat(2, 1), rat(4, 1)), rat(6, 1), "combo-6")
  175 +    if err != nil {
  176 +        return nil, err
  177 +    }
  178 +    fx.ComboResult, fx.ComboExpected, fx.DCombo6 = comboOut, comboExp, co
       mboDrv
  179 +
  180 +    // CANCEL: -3+3=0 — residual + correction numerators (1.3)+(2.1)
  181 +    // share the symbolic denominator 16pi^2; the machine checks the
  182 +    // rational cancellation, not the analysis (SK-2).
  183 +    _, cancelOut, cancelExp, cancelDrv, err := arithIdentity(store,
  184 +        physmath.Add(neg(rat(3, 1)), rat(3, 1)), rat(0, 1), "cancel")
  185 +    if err != nil {
  186 +        return nil, err
  187 +    }
  188 +    fx.CancelResult, fx.CancelExpected, fx.DCancel = cancelOut, cancelExp
       , cancelDrv
  189 +
  190 +    // HALF: (1/2)*6=3 — the (D1) one-half applied to the (1.7) six,
  191 +    // yielding the residual numerator.
  192 +    _, halfOut, halfExp, halfDrv, err := arithIdentity(store,
  193 +        physmath.Mul(rat(1, 2), rat(6, 1)), rat(3, 1), "half")
  194 +    if err != nil {
  195 +        return nil, err
  196 +    }
  197 +    fx.HalfResult, fx.HalfExpected, fx.DHalf = halfOut, halfExp, halfDrv
  198 +
  199 +    // TRACE: -3/8+3/4=+3/8 — net geometric trace coefficient (E2) in
  200 +    // units of [v1]/pi^2: remainder trace plus correction trace.
  201 +    _, traceOut, traceExp, traceDrv, err := arithIdentity(store,
  202 +        physmath.Add(neg(rat(3, 8)), rat(3, 4)), rat(3, 8), "trace")
  203 +    if err != nil {
  204 +        return nil, err
  205 +    }
  206 +    fx.TraceResult, fx.TraceExpected, fx.DTrace = traceOut, traceExp, tra
       ceDrv
  207 +
  208 +    // FLAT-CHAIN: (1/8)*(-3/8)=-3/64 — (1.9) rational core: flat [v1]
  209 +    // rational part times the (1.8) rational part.
  210 +    _, flatOut, flatExp, flatDrv, err := arithIdentity(store,
  211 +        physmath.Mul(rat(1, 8), neg(rat(3, 8))), neg(rat(3, 64)), "flat-c
       hain")
  212 +    if err != nil {
  213 +        return nil, err
  214 +    }
  215 +    fx.FlatResult, fx.FlatExpected, fx.DFlat = flatOut, flatExp, flatDrv
  216 +
  217 +    // NONZERO: -3/64 != 0 — arithmetic core of the homogeneous-W
  218 +    // falsification (1.9) (symbolic m^4/pi^2 documented in AGENTS.md).
  219 +    nz, err := put(neg(rat(3, 64)))
  220 +    if err != nil {
  221 +        return nil, err
  222 +    }
  223 +    zero, err := put(rat(0, 1))
  224 +    if err != nil {
  225 +        return nil, err
  226 +    }
  227 +    nzCanon, err := applyRule(store, physmath.RuleExprCanon, nz)
  228 +    if err != nil {
  229 +        return nil, err
  230 +    }
  231 +    zeroCanon, err := applyRule(store, physmath.RuleExprCanon, zero)
  232 +    if err != nil {
  233 +        return nil, err
  234 +    }
  235 +    rawNZ, _ := resolvePayload(store, nzCanon)
  236 +    rawZero, _ := resolvePayload(store, zeroCanon)
  237 +    if bytes.Equal(rawNZ, rawZero) {
  238 +        return nil, fmt.Errorf("nonzero gate: -3/64 canon-collided with 0
       ")
  239 +    }
  240 +    fx.NonzeroRef, fx.ZeroRef = nzCanon, zeroCanon
  241 +
  242 +    m2 := sym(DisplayM2, 0)
  243 +    R := sym(DisplayR, 1)
  244 +    // V0-SHAPE: m^2/2 - R/12 (blind (V0)); anti: Agent B's R/12 (C5).
  245 +    if fx.V0Shape, err = put(physmath.Add(
  246 +        physmath.Mul(rat(1, 2), m2),
  247 +        physmath.Mul(neg(rat(1, 12)), R))); err != nil {
  248 +        return nil, err
  249 +    }
  250 +    if fx.V0AntiB, err = put(physmath.Mul(rat(1, 12), R)); err != nil {
  251 +        return nil, err
  252 +    }
  253 +    // H1-SHAPE: 2RR - (1/2)gR^2 + 2(gBox-NN)R (blind (3.1)); anti:
  254 +    // Agent B's sign-flipped derivative terms (D2).
  255 +    Rmn := sym(DisplayRmn, 2)
  256 +    g := sym(DisplayG, 3)
  257 +    R2 := sym(DisplayR2, 4)
  258 +    BoxR := sym(DisplayBoxR, 5)
  259 +    NNR := sym(DisplayNNR, 6)
  260 +    if fx.H1Shape, err = put(physmath.Add(
  261 +        physmath.Mul(rat(2, 1), R, Rmn),
  262 +        physmath.Mul(neg(rat(1, 2)), g, R2),
  263 +        physmath.Mul(rat(2, 1), g, BoxR),
  264 +        physmath.Mul(neg(rat(2, 1)), NNR))); err != nil {
  265 +        return nil, err
  266 +    }
  267 +    if fx.H1AntiB, err = put(physmath.Add(
  268 +        physmath.Mul(rat(2, 1), R, Rmn),
  269 +        physmath.Mul(neg(rat(1, 2)), g, R2),
  270 +        physmath.Mul(neg(rat(2, 1)), g, BoxR),
  271 +        physmath.Mul(rat(2, 1), NNR))); err != nil {
  272 +        return nil, err
  273 +    }
  274 +    // H2-SHAPE: blind (3.2) unsimplified form; anti: Agent B's
  275 +    // derivative structure (D3).
  276 +    DNu := sym(DisplayDNu, 7)
  277 +    DMu := sym(DisplayDMu, 8)
  278 +    BoxRmn := sym(DisplayBoxRmn, 9)
  279 +    RAbsq := sym(DisplayRAbsq, 10)
  280 +    RR := sym(DisplayRR, 11)
  281 +    if fx.H2Shape, err = put(physmath.Add(
  282 +        neg(DNu), neg(DMu), BoxRmn,
  283 +        physmath.Mul(rat(1, 2), g, BoxR),
  284 +        physmath.Mul(neg(rat(1, 2)), g, RAbsq),
  285 +        physmath.Mul(rat(2, 1), RR))); err != nil {
  286 +        return nil, err
  287 +    }
  288 +    RiemC := sym(DisplayRiemC, 12)
  289 +    if fx.H2AntiB, err = put(physmath.Add(
  290 +        NNR,
  291 +        physmath.Mul(neg(rat(1, 1)), g, BoxR),
  292 +        neg(BoxRmn),
  293 +        physmath.Mul(rat(2, 1), RiemC),
  294 +        physmath.Mul(neg(rat(1, 2)), g, RAbsq))); err != nil {
  295 +        return nil, err
  296 +    }
  297 +    // FLRW-001: H^(1)_00 = 18(2HddH - Hd^2 + 6HdH^2), hand-derived in
  298 +    // doc 05 G4 from (3.1). Pinned golden (regression, not proof).
  299 +    HddH := sym(DisplayHddH, 13)
  300 +    Hd2 := sym(DisplayHd2, 14)
  301 +    HdH2 := sym(DisplayHdH2, 15)
  302 +    if fx.FLRW001, err = put(physmath.Mul(rat(18, 1), physmath.Add(
  303 +        physmath.Mul(rat(2, 1), HddH),
  304 +        neg(Hd2),
  305 +        physmath.Mul(rat(6, 1), HdH2)))); err != nil {
  306 +        return nil, err
  307 +    }
  308 +
  309 +    if err := checkSeparation(store, fx.V0Shape, fx.V0AntiB, "v0"); err !
       = nil {
  310 +        return nil, err
  311 +    }
  312 +    if err := checkSeparation(store, fx.H1Shape, fx.H1AntiB, "h1"); err !
       = nil {
  313 +        return nil, err
  314 +    }
  315 +    if err := checkSeparation(store, fx.H2Shape, fx.H2AntiB, "h2"); err !
       = nil {
  316 +        return nil, err
  317 +    }
  318 +
  319 +    fx.Sketch = BuildSketch()
  320 +    return fx, nil
  321 +}
  322 +
  323 +// checkSeparation gates that a golden shape and its anti-golden have
  324 +// distinct canonical payloads (regression encoding of a falsification).
  325 +func checkSeparation(store physmath.Store, shape, anti artifact.ArtifactR
       ef, tag string) error {
  326 +    cs, err := applyRule(store, physmath.RuleExprCanon, shape)
  327 +    if err != nil {
  328 +        return fmt.Errorf("%s canon shape: %w", tag, err)
  329 +    }
  330 +    ca, err := applyRule(store, physmath.RuleExprCanon, anti)
  331 +    if err != nil {
  332 +        return fmt.Errorf("%s canon anti: %w", tag, err)
  333 +    }
  334 +    rawS, err := resolvePayload(store, cs)
  335 +    if err != nil {
  336 +        return err
  337 +    }
  338 +    rawA, err := resolvePayload(store, ca)
  339 +    if err != nil {
  340 +        return err
  341 +    }
  342 +    if bytes.Equal(rawS, rawA) {
  343 +        return fmt.Errorf("%s separation: anti-golden canon-collided with
        golden", tag)
  344 +    }
  345 +    return nil
  346 +}
  347 +
  348 +func gateEqual(store physmath.Store, got, want artifact.ArtifactRef, tag
       string) error {
  349 +    rawGot, err := resolvePayload(store, got)
  350 +    if err != nil {
  351 +        return fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", tag, err)
  352 +    }
  353 +    rawWant, err := resolvePayload(store, want)
  354 +    if err != nil {
  355 +        return fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", tag, err)
  356 +    }
  357 +    if !bytes.Equal(rawGot, rawWant) {
  358 +        return fmt.Errorf("%s: payload bytes differ", tag)
  359 +    }
  360 +    return nil
  361 +}
  362 +
  363 +// Verify replays every derivation and re-checks every gate from the
  364 +// stored references. It verifies the fixture's own regression content —
  365 +// replay here is not proof of QSG-T001 (see SK-1).
  366 +func Verify(fx *Fixture) error {
  367 +    for tag, ref := range map[string]artifact.ArtifactRef{
  368 +        "combo-6": fx.DCombo6, "cancel": fx.DCancel, "half": fx.DHalf,
  369 +        "trace": fx.DTrace, "flat-chain": fx.DFlat,
  370 +    } {
  371 +        _, raw, err := fx.Store.Lookup(ref)
  372 +        if err != nil {
  373 +            return fmt.Errorf("%s lookup: %w", tag, err)
  374 +        }
  375 +        var drv physmath.Derivation
  376 +        // Decode via re-encode round trip through Validate: decode by
  377 +        // re-parsing is unnecessary — Replay takes the struct, so look
  378 +        // the derivation up through a fresh decode path below.
  379 +        _ = raw
  380 +        _ = drv
  381 +        _ = tag
  382 +        break
  383 +    }
  384 +    return verifyWithDecoded(fx)
  385 +}
  386 +
  387 +func verifyWithDecoded(fx *Fixture) error {
  388 +    for _, item := range []struct {
  389 +        tag string
  390 +        ref artifact.ArtifactRef
  391 +    }{
  392 +        {"combo-6", fx.DCombo6}, {"cancel", fx.DCancel}, {"half", fx.DHal
       f},
  393 +        {"trace", fx.DTrace}, {"flat-chain", fx.DFlat},
  394 +    } {
  395 +        if err := replayStored(fx.Store, item.ref, item.tag); err != nil
       {
  396 +            return err
  397 +        }
  398 +    }
  399 +    if err := gateEqual(fx.Store, fx.ComboResult, fx.ComboExpected, "comb
       o-6"); err != nil {
  400 +        return err
  401 +    }
  402 +    if err := gateEqual(fx.Store, fx.CancelResult, fx.CancelExpected, "ca
       ncel"); err != nil {
  403 +        return err
  404 +    }
  405 +    if err := gateEqual(fx.Store, fx.HalfResult, fx.HalfExpected, "half")
       ; err != nil {
  406 +        return err
  407 +    }
  408 +    if err := gateEqual(fx.Store, fx.TraceResult, fx.TraceExpected, "trac
       e"); err != nil {
  409 +        return err
  410 +    }
  411 +    if err := gateEqual(fx.Store, fx.FlatResult, fx.FlatExpected, "flat-c
       hain"); err != nil {
  412 +        return err
  413 +    }
  414 +    rawNZ, err := resolvePayload(fx.Store, fx.NonzeroRef)
  415 +    if err != nil {
  416 +        return err
  417 +    }
  418 +    rawZero, err := resolvePayload(fx.Store, fx.ZeroRef)
  419 +    if err != nil {
  420 +        return err
  421 +    }
  422 +    if bytes.Equal(rawNZ, rawZero) {
  423 +        return fmt.Errorf("nonzero gate: collision")
  424 +    }
  425 +    if err := checkSeparation(fx.Store, fx.V0Shape, fx.V0AntiB, "v0"); er
       r != nil {
  426 +        return err
  427 +    }
  428 +    if err := checkSeparation(fx.Store, fx.H1Shape, fx.H1AntiB, "h1"); er
       r != nil {
  429 +        return err
  430 +    }
  431 +    if err := checkSeparation(fx.Store, fx.H2Shape, fx.H2AntiB, "h2"); er
       r != nil {
  432 +        return err
  433 +    }
  434 +    for _, s := range fx.Sketch.Steps {
  435 +        if s.Status != StatusSketchUnsupported {
  436 +            return fmt.Errorf("sketch %s: status %q is not %q", s.ID, s.S
       tatus, StatusSketchUnsupported)
  437 +        }
  438 +    }
  439 +    return nil
  440 +}
  441 +
  442 +// replayStored decodes a stored derivation through the canonical codec
  443 +// path and replays it. Decoding reuses physmath's own decoder surface
  444 +// via a Validate-guarded re-mint: the bytes on record must decode to
  445 +// the identical step sequence, else replay fails.
  446 +func replayStored(store physmath.Store, ref artifact.ArtifactRef, tag str
       ing) error {
  447 +    _, raw, err := store.Lookup(ref)
  448 +    if err != nil {
  449 +        return fmt.Errorf("%s lookup: %w", tag, err)
  450 +    }
  451 +    drv, err := decodeDerivation(raw)
  452 +    if err != nil {
  453 +        return fmt.Errorf("%s decode: %w", tag, err)
  454 +    }
  455 +    if err := physmath.Replay(store, drv, map[artifact.ArtifactRef]bool{}
       ); err != nil {
  456 +        return fmt.Errorf("%s replay: %w", tag, err)
  457 +    }
  458 +    return nil
  459 +}

◆ Rewriting fixture.go cleanly (struct-carried derivations instead of a decoder,
  no scaffolding).

       us: StatusSketchUnsupported},
   65 +    }}
   66 +}
   67 +
   68 +// Fixture holds the store plus every pinned reference and the derivation
   69 +// structs (carried so Verify replays without a bespoke codec; stored
   70 +// bytes are still checked by re-encode comparison). All gates compare
   71 +// resolved payload bytes, never bare references.
   72 +type Fixture struct {
   73 +    Store physmath.Store
   74 +
   75 +    DCombo6, DCancel, DHalf, DTrace, DFlat artifact.ArtifactRef
   76 +    DCombo6Deriv, DCancelDeriv, DHalfDeriv, DTraceDeriv, DFlatDeriv physm
       ath.Derivation
   77 +
   78 +    ComboResult, ComboExpected   artifact.ArtifactRef
   79 +    CancelResult, CancelExpected artifact.ArtifactRef
   80 +    HalfResult, HalfExpected     artifact.ArtifactRef
   81 +    TraceResult, TraceExpected   artifact.ArtifactRef
   82 +    FlatResult, FlatExpected     artifact.ArtifactRef
   83 +    NonzeroRef, ZeroRef          artifact.ArtifactRef
   84 +
   85 +    V0Shape, V0AntiB artifact.ArtifactRef
   86 +    H1Shape, H1AntiB artifact.ArtifactRef
   87 +    H2Shape, H2AntiB artifact.ArtifactRef
   88 +    FLRW001          artifact.ArtifactRef
   89 +
   90 +    Sketch QSGSketch
   91 +}
   92 +
   93 +func resolvePayload(store physmath.Store, ref artifact.ArtifactRef) ([]by
       te, error) {
   94 +    _, payload, err := store.Lookup(ref)
   95 +    return payload, err
   96 +}
   97 +
   98 +func applyRule(store physmath.Store, rule string, inputs ...artifact.Arti
       factRef) (artifact.ArtifactRef, error) {
   99 +    res, err := physmath.Apply(store, physmath.Invocation{
  100 +        ProducerNamespace: "physmath",
  101 +        RuleID:            rule,
  102 +        InputRefs:         inputs,
  103 +    })
  104 +    if err != nil {
  105 +        return artifact.ArtifactRef{}, err
  106 +    }
  107 +    return res.OutputRef, nil
  108 +}
  109 +
  110 +func sym(display string, ordinal uint32) physmath.Expr {
  111 +    return physmath.NewBoundSymbol(display, physmath.DeriveParameterID(or
       dinal))
  112 +}
  113 +
  114 +func rat(num, den int64) physmath.Expr { return physmath.MustRational(num
       , den) }
  115 +func neg(e physmath.Expr) physmath.Expr { return physmath.Neg(e) }
  116 +
  117 +// arithIdentity mints premise input, simplifies it, gates the output
  118 +// against expected, and packages the single-step derivation.
  119 +func arithIdentity(store physmath.Store, input, expected physmath.Expr, t
       ag string) (in, out, exp, drvRef artifact.ArtifactRef, drv physmath.Deriv
       ation, err error) {
  120 +    if in, err = physmath.PutExpression(store, input); err != nil {
  121 +        return in, out, exp, drvRef, drv, fmt.Errorf("%s put input: %w",
       tag, err)
  122 +    }
  123 +    out, err = applyRule(store, physmath.RuleExprSimplify, in)
  124 +    if err != nil {
  125 +        return in, out, exp, drvRef, drv, fmt.Errorf("%s simplify: %w", t
       ag, err)
  126 +    }
  127 +    if exp, err = physmath.PutExpression(store, expected); err != nil {
  128 +        return in, out, exp, drvRef, drv, fmt.Errorf("%s put expected: %w
       ", tag, err)
  129 +    }
  130 +    rawOut, err := resolvePayload(store, out)
  131 +    if err != nil {
  132 +        return in, out, exp, drvRef, drv, fmt.Errorf("%s resolve out: %w"
       , tag, err)
  133 +    }
  134 +    rawExp, err := resolvePayload(store, exp)
  135 +    if err != nil {
  136 +        return in, out, exp, drvRef, drv, fmt.Errorf("%s resolve exp: %w"
       , tag, err)
  137 +    }
  138 +    if !bytes.Equal(rawOut, rawExp) {
  139 +        return in, out, exp, drvRef, drv, fmt.Errorf("%s gate: output byt
       es differ", tag)
  140 +    }
  141 +    drv = physmath.Derivation{
  142 +        PremiseRefs: []artifact.ArtifactRef{in},
  143 +        Steps: []physmath.DerivationStep{
  144 +            {Ordinal: 0, ProducerNamespace: "physmath", RuleID: physmath.
       RuleExprSimplify, InputRefs: []artifact.ArtifactRef{in}, OutputRef: out},
  145 +        },
  146 +        ResultRefs: []artifact.ArtifactRef{out},
  147 +    }
  148 +    if err := drv.Validate(); err != nil {
  149 +        return in, out, exp, drvRef, drv, fmt.Errorf("%s validate: %w", t
       ag, err)
  150 +    }
  151 +    raw, err := drv.Encode()
  152 +    if err != nil {
  153 +        return in, out, exp, drvRef, drv, fmt.Errorf("%s encode: %w", tag
       , err)
  154 +    }
  155 +    if drvRef, err = store.Put(physmath.FamilyDerivation, raw); err != ni
       l {
  156 +        return in, out, exp, drvRef, drv, fmt.Errorf("%s store: %w", tag,
        err)
  157 +    }
  158 +    return in, out, exp, drvRef, drv, nil
  159 +}
  160 +
  161 +// Build mints every golden, derivation, and the sketch. Hand-derivation
  162 +// sources: doc 04 (1.7) for COMBO-6, (1.3)+(2.1) for CANCEL, (D1) for
  163 +// HALF, (3.4/E2) for TRACE, (1.9) for FLAT-CHAIN and NONZERO, (V0)/(3.1)
       /
  164 +// (3.2) for shapes, doc 05 G4 for FLRW-001.
  165 +func Build() (*Fixture, error) {
  166 +    store := physmath.NewMemStore()
  167 +    fx := &Fixture{Store: store}
  168 +    put := func(e physmath.Expr) (artifact.ArtifactRef, error) {
  169 +        return physmath.PutExpression(store, e)
  170 +    }
  171 +
  172 +    // COMBO-6: 2+4=6 — the (1.7) decomposition (2V1/sigma part + 4V1
  173 +    // transport part). Rational skeleton only; the analysis is SK-3.
  174 +    _, comboOut, comboExp, comboDrv, comboDeriv, err := arithIdentity(sto
       re,
  175 +        physmath.Add(rat(2, 1), rat(4, 1)), rat(6, 1), "combo-6")
  176 +    if err != nil {
  177 +        return nil, err
  178 +    }
  179 +    fx.ComboResult, fx.ComboExpected, fx.DCombo6, fx.DCombo6Deriv = combo
       Out, comboExp, comboDrv, comboDeriv
  180 +
  181 +    // CANCEL: -3+3=0 — residual + correction numerators (1.3)+(2.1)
  182 +    // share the symbolic denominator 16pi^2; the machine checks the
  183 +    // rational cancellation, not the analysis (SK-2).
  184 +    _, cancelOut, cancelExp, cancelDrv, cancelDeriv, err := arithIdentity
       (store,
  185 +        physmath.Add(neg(rat(3, 1)), rat(3, 1)), rat(0, 1), "cancel")
  186 +    if err != nil {
  187 +        return nil, err
  188 +    }
  189 +    fx.CancelResult, fx.CancelExpected, fx.DCancel, fx.DCancelDeriv = can
       celOut, cancelExp, cancelDrv, cancelDeriv
  190 +
  191 +    // HALF: (1/2)*6=3 — the (D1) one-half applied to the (1.7) six,
  192 +    // yielding the residual numerator.
  193 +    _, halfOut, halfExp, halfDrv, halfDeriv, err := arithIdentity(store,
  194 +        physmath.Mul(rat(1, 2), rat(6, 1)), rat(3, 1), "half")
  195 +    if err != nil {
  196 +        return nil, err
  197 +    }
  198 +    fx.HalfResult, fx.HalfExpected, fx.DHalf, fx.DHalfDeriv = halfOut, ha
       lfExp, halfDrv, halfDeriv
  199 +
  200 +    // TRACE: -3/8+3/4=+3/8 — net geometric trace coefficient (E2) in
  201 +    // units of [v1]/pi^2: remainder trace plus correction trace.
  202 +    _, traceOut, traceExp, traceDrv, traceDeriv, err := arithIdentity(sto
       re,
  203 +        physmath.Add(neg(rat(3, 8)), rat(3, 4)), rat(3, 8), "trace")
  204 +    if err != nil {
  205 +        return nil, err
  206 +    }
  207 +    fx.TraceResult, fx.TraceExpected, fx.DTrace, fx.DTraceDeriv = traceOu
       t, traceExp, traceDrv, traceDeriv
  208 +
  209 +    // FLAT-CHAIN: (1/8)*(-3/8)=-3/64 — (1.9) rational core: flat [v1]
  210 +    // rational part times the (1.8) rational part.
  211 +    _, flatOut, flatExp, flatDrv, flatDeriv, err := arithIdentity(store,
  212 +        physmath.Mul(rat(1, 8), neg(rat(3, 8))), neg(rat(3, 64)), "flat-c
       hain")
  213 +    if err != nil {
  214 +        return nil, err
  215 +    }
  216 +    fx.FlatResult, fx.FlatExpected, fx.DFlat, fx.DFlatDeriv = flatOut, fl
       atExp, flatDrv, flatDeriv
  217 +
  218 +    // NONZERO: -3/64 != 0 — arithmetic core of the homogeneous-W
  219 +    // falsification (1.9) (symbolic m^4/pi^2 documented in AGENTS.md).
  220 +    nz, err := put(neg(rat(3, 64)))
  221 +    if err != nil {
  222 +        return nil, err
  223 +    }
  224 +    zero, err := put(rat(0, 1))
  225 +    if err != nil {
  226 +        return nil, err
  227 +    }
  228 +    nzCanon, err := applyRule(store, physmath.RuleExprCanon, nz)
  229 +    if err != nil {
  230 +        return nil, err
  231 +    }
  232 +    zeroCanon, err := applyRule(store, physmath.RuleExprCanon, zero)
  233 +    if err != nil {
  234 +        return nil, err
  235 +    }
  236 +    rawNZ, _ := resolvePayload(store, nzCanon)
  237 +    rawZero, _ := resolvePayload(store, zeroCanon)
  238 +    if bytes.Equal(rawNZ, rawZero) {
  239 +        return nil, fmt.Errorf("nonzero gate: -3/64 canon-collided with 0
       ")
  240 +    }
  241 +    fx.NonzeroRef, fx.ZeroRef = nzCanon, zeroCanon
  242 +
  243 +    m2 := sym(DisplayM2, 0)
  244 +    R := sym(DisplayR, 1)
  245 +    // V0-SHAPE: m^2/2 - R/12 (blind (V0)); anti: Agent B's R/12 (C5).
  246 +    if fx.V0Shape, err = put(physmath.Add(
  247 +        physmath.Mul(rat(1, 2), m2),
  248 +        physmath.Mul(neg(rat(1, 12)), R))); err != nil {
  249 +        return nil, err
  250 +    }
  251 +    if fx.V0AntiB, err = put(physmath.Mul(rat(1, 12), R)); err != nil {
  252 +        return nil, err
  253 +    }
  254 +    // H1-SHAPE: 2RR - (1/2)gR^2 + 2(gBox-NN)R (blind (3.1)); anti:
  255 +    // Agent B's sign-flipped derivative terms (D2).
  256 +    Rmn := sym(DisplayRmn, 2)
  257 +    g := sym(DisplayG, 3)
  258 +    R2 := sym(DisplayR2, 4)
  259 +    BoxR := sym(DisplayBoxR, 5)
  260 +    NNR := sym(DisplayNNR, 6)
  261 +    if fx.H1Shape, err = put(physmath.Add(
  262 +        physmath.Mul(rat(2, 1), R, Rmn),
  263 +        physmath.Mul(neg(rat(1, 2)), g, R2),
  264 +        physmath.Mul(rat(2, 1), g, BoxR),
  265 +        physmath.Mul(neg(rat(2, 1)), NNR))); err != nil {
  266 +        return nil, err
  267 +    }
  268 +    if fx.H1AntiB, err = put(physmath.Add(
  269 +        physmath.Mul(rat(2, 1), R, Rmn),
  270 +        physmath.Mul(neg(rat(1, 2)), g, R2),
  271 +        physmath.Mul(neg(rat(2, 1)), g, BoxR),
  272 +        physmath.Mul(rat(2, 1), NNR))); err != nil {
  273 +        return nil, err
  274 +    }
  275 +    // H2-SHAPE: blind (3.2) unsimplified form; anti: Agent B's
  276 +    // derivative structure (D3).
  277 +    DNu := sym(DisplayDNu, 7)
  278 +    DMu := sym(DisplayDMu, 8)
  279 +    BoxRmn := sym(DisplayBoxRmn, 9)
  280 +    RAbsq := sym(DisplayRAbsq, 10)
  281 +    RR := sym(DisplayRR, 11)
  282 +    if fx.H2Shape, err = put(physmath.Add(
  283 +        neg(DNu), neg(DMu), BoxRmn,
  284 +        physmath.Mul(rat(1, 2), g, BoxR),
  285 +        physmath.Mul(neg(rat(1, 2)), g, RAbsq),
  286 +        physmath.Mul(rat(2, 1), RR))); err != nil {
  287 +        return nil, err
  288 +    }
  289 +    RiemC := sym(DisplayRiemC, 12)
  290 +    if fx.H2AntiB, err = put(physmath.Add(
  291 +        NNR,
  292 +        physmath.Mul(neg(rat(1, 1)), g, BoxR),
  293 +        neg(BoxRmn),
  294 +        physmath.Mul(rat(2, 1), RiemC),
  295 +        physmath.Mul(neg(rat(1, 2)), g, RAbsq))); err != nil {
  296 +        return nil, err
  297 +    }
  298 +    // FLRW-001: H^(1)_00 = 18(2HddH - Hd^2 + 6HdH^2), hand-derived in
  299 +    // doc 05 G4 from (3.1). Pinned golden (regression, not proof).
  300 +    HddH := sym(DisplayHddH, 13)
  301 +    Hd2 := sym(DisplayHd2, 14)
  302 +    HdH2 := sym(DisplayHdH2, 15)
  303 +    if fx.FLRW001, err = put(physmath.Mul(rat(18, 1), physmath.Add(
  304 +        physmath.Mul(rat(2, 1), HddH),
  305 +        neg(Hd2),
  306 +        physmath.Mul(rat(6, 1), HdH2)))); err != nil {
  307 +        return nil, err
  308 +    }
  309 +
  310 +    if err := checkSeparation(store, fx.V0Shape, fx.V0AntiB, "v0"); err !
       = nil {
  311 +        return nil, err
  312 +    }
  313 +    if err := checkSeparation(store, fx.H1Shape, fx.H1AntiB, "h1"); err !
       = nil {
  314 +        return nil, err
  315 +    }
  316 +    if err := checkSeparation(store, fx.H2Shape, fx.H2AntiB, "h2"); err !
       = nil {
  317 +        return nil, err
  318 +    }
  319 +
  320 +    fx.Sketch = BuildSketch()
  321 +    return fx, nil
  322 +}
  323 +
  324 +// checkSeparation gates that a golden shape and its anti-golden have
  325 +// distinct canonical payloads (regression encoding of a falsification).
  326 +func checkSeparation(store physmath.Store, shape, anti artifact.ArtifactR
       ef, tag string) error {
  327 +    cs, err := applyRule(store, physmath.RuleExprCanon, shape)
  328 +    if err != nil {
  329 +        return fmt.Errorf("%s canon shape: %w", tag, err)
  330 +    }
  331 +    ca, err := applyRule(store, physmath.RuleExprCanon, anti)
  332 +    if err != nil {
  333 +        return fmt.Errorf("%s canon anti: %w", tag, err)
  334 +    }
  335 +    rawS, err := resolvePayload(store, cs)
  336 +    if err != nil {
  337 +        return err
  338 +    }
  339 +    rawA, err := resolvePayload(store, ca)
  340 +    if err != nil {
  341 +        return err
  342 +    }
  343 +    if bytes.Equal(rawS, rawA) {
  344 +        return fmt.Errorf("%s separation: anti-golden canon-collided with
        golden", tag)
  345 +    }
  346 +    return nil
  347 +}
  348 +
  349 +func gateEqual(store physmath.Store, got, want artifact.ArtifactRef, tag
       string) error {
  350 +    rawGot, err := resolvePayload(store, got)
  351 +    if err != nil {
  352 +        return fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", tag, err)
  353 +    }
  354 +    rawWant, err := resolvePayload(store, want)
  355 +    if err != nil {
  356 +        return fmt.Errorf("%s: %w", tag, err)
  357 +    }
  358 +    if !bytes.Equal(rawGot, rawWant) {
  359 +        return fmt.Errorf("%s: payload bytes differ", tag)
  360 +    }
  361 +    return nil
  362 +}
  363 +
  364 +// Verify replays every derivation (checking stored bytes by re-encode)
  365 +// and re-checks every gate from the stored references. It verifies the
  366 +// fixture's own regression content — replay here is not proof of
  367 +// QSG-T001 (see SK-1).
  368 +func Verify(fx *Fixture) error {
  369 +    for _, item := range []struct {
  370 +        tag string
  371 +        ref artifact.ArtifactRef
  372 +        drv physmath.Derivation
  373 +    }{
  374 +        {"combo-6", fx.DCombo6, fx.DCombo6Deriv},
  375 +        {"cancel", fx.DCancel, fx.DCancelDeriv},
  376 +        {"half", fx.DHalf, fx.DHalfDeriv},
  377 +        {"trace", fx.DTrace, fx.DTraceDeriv},
  378 +        {"flat-chain", fx.DFlat, fx.DFlatDeriv},
  379 +    } {
  380 +        stored, err := resolvePayload(fx.Store, item.ref)
  381 +        if err != nil {
  382 +            return fmt.Errorf("%s lookup: %w", item.tag, err)
  383 +        }
  384 +        enc, err := item.drv.Encode()
  385 +        if err != nil {
  386 +            return fmt.Errorf("%s encode: %w", item.tag, err)
  387 +        }
  388 +        if !bytes.Equal(stored, enc) {
  389 +            return fmt.Errorf("%s: stored derivation bytes differ from re
       -encode", item.tag)
  390 +        }
  391 +        if err := physmath.Replay(fx.Store, item.drv, map[artifact.Artifa
       ctRef]bool{}); err != nil {
  392 +            return fmt.Errorf("%s replay: %w", item.tag, err)
  393 +        }
  394 +    }
  395 +    if err := gateEqual(fx.Store, fx.ComboResult, fx.ComboExpected, "comb
       o-6"); err != nil {
  396 +        return err
  397 +    }
  398 +    if err := gateEqual(fx.Store, fx.CancelResult, fx.CancelExpected, "ca
       ncel"); err != nil {
  399 +        return err
  400 +    }
  401 +    if err := gateEqual(fx.Store, fx.HalfResult, fx.HalfExpected, "half")
       ; err != nil {
  402 +        return err
  403 +    }
  404 +    if err := gateEqual(fx.Store, fx.TraceResult, fx.TraceExpected, "trac
       e"); err != nil {
  405 +        return err
  406 +    }
  407 +    if err := gateEqual(fx.Store, fx.FlatResult, fx.FlatExpected, "flat-c
       hain"); err != nil {
  408 +        return err
  409 +    }
  410 +    rawNZ, err := resolvePayload(fx.Store, fx.NonzeroRef)
  411 +    if err != nil {
  412 +        return err
  413 +    }
  414 +    rawZero, err := resolvePayload(fx.Store, fx.ZeroRef)
  415 +    if err != nil {
  416 +        return err
  417 +    }
  418 +    if bytes.Equal(rawNZ, rawZero) {
  419 +        return fmt.Errorf("nonzero gate: collision")
  420 +    }
  421 +    if err := checkSeparation(fx.Store, fx.V0Shape, fx.V0AntiB, "v0"); er
       r != nil {
  422 +        return err
  423 +    }
  424 +    if err := checkSeparation(fx.Store, fx.H1Shape, fx.H1AntiB, "h1"); er
       r != nil {
  425 +        return err
  426 +    }
  427 +    if err := checkSeparation(fx.Store, fx.H2Shape, fx.H2AntiB, "h2"); er
       r != nil {
  428 +        return err
  429 +    }
  430 +    for _, s := range fx.Sketch.Steps {
  431 +        if s.Status != StatusSketchUnsupported {
  432 +            return fmt.Errorf("sketch %s: status %q is not %q", s.ID, s.S
       tatus, StatusSketchUnsupported)
  433 +        }
  434 +    }
  435 +    return nil
  436 +}

◆ Wrote research/experiments/qsg-preflight-001/theorems/qsg-t001/AGENTS.md (+68)
   1 +# AGENTS.md — theorems/qsg-t001
   2 +
   3 +Minimal QSG-T001 theorem fixture. Audit: `../agents/agent-c-conservation-a
      udit/`
   4 +(docs 01–07). Theorem: doc 06 (T). This area is representation +
   5 +regression only: it pins the audit's c-number results and replays their
   6 +rational-arithmetic skeleton. It proves nothing.
   7 +
   8 +## Layout
   9 +
  10 +- `go.mod`, `go.work` — new module `github.com/PithomLabs/qsg-t001`;
  11 +  colocated workspace over phys-artifact + phys-math (absolute
  12 +  `use` paths: this module lives outside `/home/chaschel/Documents/go`).
  13 +  No phys-lib import: GR-closed framework categories must not carry
  14 +  QFT content.
  15 +- `fixture.go` — `Build` (mints goldens, five one-step SIMPLIFY
  16 +  derivations, six-step sketch) and `Verify` (replays + payload-byte
  17 +  gates). All gates compare resolved payload bytes, never bare refs.
  18 +- `manifest.json` — 16-key package manifest (equivariance shape, QSG
  19 +  content, locally validated; the GR manifestschema gate does not
  20 +  apply to QFT content).
  21 +- `research_metadata.json` — claims/obligations sidecar (ltb shape).
  22 +  `math_status` vs `machine_status` are separate fields: machine
  23 +  execution never upgrades a mathematical disposition.
  24 +- `protocol/derivation_test.go` — executable suite (this file reads the
  25 +  JSON sidecars; fixture.go does not).
  26 +
  27 +## Commands (GOCACHE is read-only; always override)
  28 +
  29 +```
  30 +export GOWORK=$PWD/go.work GOCACHE=/tmp/qsg-preflight/gocache GOPROXY=off
  31 +go build ./...
  32 +go test -count=1 ./...
  33 +go vet ./...
  34 +gofmt -l .   # must print nothing
  35 +```
  36 +
  37 +## What replay proves (and does not)
  38 +
  39 +Proves: five rational identities (2+4=6; -3+3=0; (1/2)*6=3;
  40 +-3/8+3/4=+3/8; (1/8)*(-3/8)=-3/64), one canon-inequality (-3/64 != 0),
  41 +byte-stability of four shape goldens, separation of three anti-goldens,
  42 +and all-unsupported sketch status. These are the audit's arithmetic
  43 +skeleton plus regression pins.
  44 +
  45 +Does NOT prove: the remainder limit, the divergence computation, the
  46 +residual, the correction, the cancellation, or (T) — those are human
  47 +proof obligations SK-1..SK-6 (see `BuildSketch`), each pointing at its
  48 +audit section. Symbolic factors (pi powers, m^4, curvature polynomials)
  49 +are documented, never computed: e.g. CANCEL checks -3+3=0 given the
  50 +shared symbolic denominator 16pi^2.
  51 +
  52 +## Golden policy
  53 +
  54 +Every golden cites the hand derivation that produced it (fixture.go
  55 +comments): (1.7), (1.3)+(2.1), (D1), (3.4/E2), (1.9), (V0), (3.1),
  56 +(3.2), doc-05 G4. Anti-goldens cite the falsified Agent B formula (C5,
  57 +D2, D3) and the rendered page inspected. Changing a golden requires
  58 +changing the cited audit section first; the test suite fails otherwise.
  59 +
  60 +## No-go rules
  61 +
  62 +- No imports beyond phys-artifact + phys-math.
  63 +- No universal claim in `expected_result` (enforced by test).
  64 +- No `Verify`/`Replay` method on `QSGSketch`, ever (enforced by test).
  65 +- No edits outside this directory (shared repos read-only; the
  66 +  committed `/home/chaschel/Documents/go/go.work` is untouched).
  67 +- No weakening a failing gate: fix the fixture or record why the
  68 +  expectation is wrong in the manifest anomalies.

   22 +func areaFile(t *testing.T, name string) []byte {
   23 +    t.Helper()
   24 +    _, thisFile, _, ok := runtime.Caller(0)
   25 +    if !ok {
   26 +        t.Fatal("runtime.Caller failed")
   27 +    }
   28 +    raw, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(thisFile), "..", n
       ame))
   29 +    if err != nil {
   30 +        t.Fatal(err)
   31 +    }
   32 +    return raw
   33 +}
   34 +
   35 +func resolve(t *testing.T, store physmath.Store, ref artifact.ArtifactRef
       ) []byte {
   36 +    t.Helper()
   37 +    _, payload, err := store.Lookup(ref)
   38 +    if err != nil {
   39 +        t.Fatal(err)
   40 +    }
   41 +    return payload
   42 +}

   54 +func TestGoldenPayloadsStable(t *testing.T) {
   55 +    a, err := qsgt001.Build()
   56 +    if err != nil {
   57 +        t.Fatal(err)
   58 +    }
   59 +    b, err := qsgt001.Build()
   60 +    if err != nil {
   61 +        t.Fatal(err)
   62 +    }
   63 +    pairs := [][2]artifact.ArtifactRef{
   64 +        {a.ComboResult, b.ComboResult},
   65 +        {a.CancelResult, b.CancelResult},
   66 +        {a.HalfResult, b.HalfResult},
   67 +        {a.TraceResult, b.TraceResult},
   68 +        {a.FlatResult, b.FlatResult},
   69 +        {a.V0Shape, b.V0Shape},
   70 +        {a.H1Shape, b.H1Shape},
   71 +        {a.H2Shape, b.H2Shape},
   72 +        {a.FLRW001, b.FLRW001},
   73 +    }
   74 +    for i, p := range pairs {
   75 +        if !bytes.Equal(resolve(t, a.Store, p[0]), resolve(t, b.Store, p[
       1])) {
   76 +            t.Fatalf("golden %d payload differs across rebuilds", i)
   77 +        }
   78 +    }
   79 +}

   81 +func TestAntiGoldenSeparation(t *testing.T) {
   82 +    fx, err := qsgt001.Build()
   83 +    if err != nil {
   84 +        t.Fatal(err)
   85 +    }
   86 +    // Build already gates separation; re-assert from stored refs so the
   87 +    // suite, not just the constructor, owns the falsification regression
       .
   88 +    for tag, pair := range map[string][2]artifact.ArtifactRef{
   89 +        "v0": {fx.V0Shape, fx.V0AntiB},
   90 +        "h1": {fx.H1Shape, fx.H1AntiB},
   91 +        "h2": {fx.H2Shape, fx.H2AntiB},
   92 +    } {
   93 +        if bytes.Equal(resolve(t, fx.Store, pair[0]), resolve(t, fx.Store
       , pair[1])) {
   94 +            t.Fatalf("%s: anti-golden payload equals golden", tag)
   95 +        }
   96 +    }
   97 +}

   99 +func TestMutations(t *testing.T) {
  100 +    fx, err := qsgt001.Build()
  101 +    if err != nil {
  102 +        t.Fatal(err)
  103 +    }
  104 +    // Mutant 1: wrong rational input (3+4 instead of 2+4) must not match
  105 +    // the COMBO-6 expected payload.
  106 +    mutantIn, err := physmath.PutExpression(fx.Store, physmath.Add(
  107 +        physmath.MustRational(3, 1), physmath.MustRational(4, 1)))
  108 +    if err != nil {
  109 +        t.Fatal(err)
  110 +    }
  111 +    res, err := physmath.Apply(fx.Store, physmath.Invocation{
  112 +        ProducerNamespace: "physmath",
  113 +        RuleID:            physmath.RuleExprSimplify,
  114 +        InputRefs:         []artifact.ArtifactRef{mutantIn},
  115 +    })
  116 +    if err != nil {
  117 +        t.Fatal(err)
  118 +    }
  119 +    if bytes.Equal(resolve(t, fx.Store, res.OutputRef), resolve(t, fx.Sto
       re, fx.ComboExpected)) {
  120 +        t.Fatal("mutant 3+4 matched the 2+4 golden")
  121 +    }
  122 +    // Mutant 2: tampered derivation bytes must fail replay.
  123 +    _, raw, err := fx.Store.Lookup(fx.DCombo6)
  124 +    if err != nil {
  125 +        t.Fatal(err)
  126 +    }
  127 +    bad := bytes.Clone(raw)
  128 +    bad[len(bad)-1] ^= 0xff
  129 +    badRef, err := fx.Store.Put(physmath.FamilyDerivation, bad)
  130 +    if err != nil {
  131 +        t.Fatal(err)
  132 +    }
  133 +    _ = badRef
  134 +    // Replay the tampered derivation by decoding through a fresh struct
  135 +    // is impossible without a codec; instead assert the tamper changed
  136 +    // the bytes on record (integrity tripwire) and that Verify on a
  137 +    // fixture whose stored derivation was swapped fails. Swap check:
  138 +    fx2, err := qsgt001.Build()
  139 +    if err != nil {
  140 +        t.Fatal(err)
  141 +    }
  142 +    fx2.DCombo6 = badRef
  143 +    if err := qsgt001.Verify(fx2); err == nil {
  144 +        t.Fatal("Verify accepted a tampered derivation ref")
  145 +    }
  146 +}

       ); ok {

  194 +func TestManifestStrict(t *testing.T) {
  195 +    raw := areaFile(t, "manifest.json")
  196 +    var mf manifestFile
  197 +    dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(raw))
  198 +    dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
  199 +    if err := dec.Decode(&mf); err != nil {
  200 +        t.Fatalf("manifest strict decode: %v", err)
  201 +    }
  202 +    if len(mf.ExpectedResult) == 0 || len(mf.Limitations) < 3 || len(mf.A
       ssumptions) == 0 {
  203 +        t.Fatal("manifest missing expected_result/limitations/assumptions
       ")
  204 +    }
  205 +    joinedExp := ""
  206 +    for _, e := range mf.ExpectedResult {
  207 +        if e.ExecutedBy == "" {
  208 +            t.Fatalf("expected_result entry without executed_by: %q", e.S
       tatement)
  209 +        }
  210 +        joinedExp += e.Statement + "\n"
  211 +    }
  212 +    if strings.Contains(joinedExp, "universal") {
  213 +        t.Fatal("expected_result must not claim the universal identity")
  214 +    }
  215 +    joinedLim := strings.Join(mf.Limitations, "\n")
  216 +    if !strings.Contains(joinedLim, "universal") {
  217 +        t.Fatal("limitations must state the universal identity is unexecu
       ted")
  218 +    }
  219 +    joinedAnom := strings.Join(mf.Anomalies, "\n")
  220 +    if !strings.Contains(joinedAnom, "E1") || !strings.Contains(joinedAno
       m, "E2") {
  221 +        t.Fatal("anomalies must record errata E1 and E2")
  222 +    }
  223 +}

  243 +func TestSidecar(t *testing.T) {
  244 +    raw := areaFile(t, "research_metadata.json")
  245 +    var sc sidecarFile
  246 +    dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(raw))
  247 +    dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
  248 +    if err := dec.Decode(&sc); err != nil {
  249 +        t.Fatalf("sidecar strict decode: %v", err)
  250 +    }
  251 +    seenTheorem := false
  252 +    for _, c := range sc.Claims {
  253 +        if strings.Contains(c.Status, "machine-proved") || strings.Contai
       ns(c.Status, "machine-verified") {
  254 +            t.Fatalf("claim %s asserts machine proof: %q", c.ID, c.Status
       )
  255 +        }
  256 +        if c.ID == "QSG-T001" {
  257 +            seenTheorem = true
  258 +            if !strings.Contains(c.Status, "machine-unexecuted") {
  259 +                t.Fatalf("QSG-T001 must be machine-unexecuted, got %q", c
       .Status)
  260 +            }
  261 +        }
  262 +    }
  263 +    if !seenTheorem {
  264 +        t.Fatal("QSG-T001 claim missing")
  265 +    }
  266 +    wantO := map[string]bool{"O1": false, "O2": false, "O3": false, "O4":
        false, "O5": false}
  267 +    for _, o := range sc.Obligations {
  268 +        if _, ok := wantO[o.ID]; !ok {
  269 +            t.Fatalf("unexpected obligation %s", o.ID)
  270 +        }
  271 +        wantO[o.ID] = true
  272 +        if o.EvidenceRequired == "" {
  273 +            t.Fatalf("obligation %s lacks evidence_required", o.ID)
  274 +        }
  275 +    }
  276 +    for id, seen := range wantO {
  277 +        if !seen {
  278 +            t.Fatalf("obligation %s missing", id)
  279 +        }
  280 +    }
  281 +}

  Goal

  Implement plans/prompt5.md (Agent A — QSG-T001 normalization repair) in light
  of plans/plan5_imp_review.md: fix the incorrectly normalized Hadamard
  subtraction, reconstruct the conservation calculation from corrected
  definitions, determine whether the reported correction coefficient survives,
  and reconcile all theorem/fixture metadata — without restarting the programme,
  rewriting recon, or adding infrastructure.

  Success Criteria

  Exactly the prompt5 §7 checklist — the task is complete only when:
  singularities match exactly; the remainder is genuinely smooth; the
  point-split limit is well-defined; the remainder equation is independently
  derived; the residual is explicitly calculated; the correction is derived, not
  imported; its divergence is calculated; cancellation is demonstrated; source
  correspondence is reconciled or explicitly BLOCKED; all metadata agree; tests
  demonstrate the limited capabilities claimed. Any failed math gate → BLOCKED,
  theorem not promoted.

  Approach

  Triage first (my session check, not a result): I verified the reviewer's
  singularity arithmetic two independent ways — the Minkowski Wightman mode
  integral gives G⁺ ~ 1/(8π²σ), and DF's Feynman form (inspected in doc 03)
  gives the same — so G⁽¹⁾ ~ 1/(4π²σ) vs H⁽¹⁾ ~ 1/(8π²σ), and S as written is
  singular. The defect is real; P2 is withdrawn. The full independent
  verification is Step 1 itself.

  Key decisions:

  • Prescription choice: recommend Alternative A (S = (1/2)G⁽¹⁾ − H⁽¹⁾, standard
    H kept) pending Step-1 proof — minimal diff to locked §0.3, matches DF/BD
    H-normalization. A-vs-B looks notationally equivalent (same S either way);
    Step 1 must prove that or find the difference. Decided by evidence in-step,
    not assumed.
  • What transfers: the D1/TI divergence proof structure (a cross-checked
    conditional: if S smooth with equation J, then residual = (1/2)∇[J]) may be
    reused only after step-by-step re-verification against the new S — the old
    steps are vacuous (old S singular), so nothing is copy-pasted. L2 must be
    derived fresh (transport equations stated and proved), per review §3 /
    prompt5 §3.3 — no import at the crucial point.
  • C2 reversal anticipated: with correct normalizations, Agent B's W = G⁺ − H
    has smooth real part (B's subtraction captures the right singularity); B's
    defect restates as the unaddressed distributional imaginary part plus the
    false homogeneous equation — not singularity. Step 5 must verify this
    explicitly; the reversal is recorded openly, never silent.
  • No coefficient preview in this plan: any scaling guess from the old
    (vacuous) derivation is forbidden as a target. Reconstruction is the only
    route; the old 3/(16π²) is treated as unestablished ink.
  • Blocking semantics per review §4: O1 open with explicitly decided
    significance; O2 blocks DF-equivalence claims, not the internal theorem; O5
    never blocks the mathematical theorem. Reconciled across 06/07/JSON.
  • Fixture frozen until corrected math gates pass; then goldens/metadata only,
    architecture intact, SK-steps unsupported.

  Steps

  1. Normalization verification (prompt5 §§1–2). From the Minkowski mode
     integral plus DF correspondence (re-inspected, not recalled): verify
     Wightman, symmetric-function, and Feynman singular coefficients
     independently; prove or refute A⟷B equivalence; freeze the corrected
     prescription with derivations. Gate: singularities match exactly.
  2. Remainder reconstruction ("03-repair", prompt5 §3.1–3.4). Prove S_new
     smooth; re-derive its inhomogeneous KG equation; state and derive the
     required Hadamard transport equations (closes L2); establish coincidence
     limits. Gate: well-defined point-split limit.
  3. Divergence reconstruction ("04-repair", prompt5 §3.5–3.9). Re-execute D1/TI
     for S_new with every Synge instance shown; derive residual, force
     correction from residual, compute its divergence, demonstrate cancellation.
     No old coefficient reused. Gate: explicit residual → correction →
     cancellation.
  4. Source + A-term audit (prompt5 §3.10, §4). DF/Wald correspondence under
     corrected definitions; A smoothness and field-equation limit computed —
     reconcile or mark BLOCKED. Either outcome acceptable; unspecified smooth
     terms forbidden as cover.
  5. Finding repair. Correct doc-05 C2 (reversed arithmetic, restated defect)
     and C1; check every other 05 verdict for normalization-dependence (expected
     unaffected: C3–C7, D–J — verify each, assume none); restate E4 for S_new.
  6. Provenance + metadata repair (prompt5 §5, review §§1/4/5). Append errata
     (locks byte-intact); rewrite 06 (theorem, coefficients, re-answered gate,
     O1–O5 per §4 distinctions); fix 07's status/chronology contradiction and
     verdict; reconcile research_metadata.json + manifest.json blocking
     semantics; rewrite Phys-Pseudo with complete premise lists (field-equation,
     geometric, operator, Synge, transport premises all named).
  7. Fixture repair (prompt5 §6, review §5). Update affected
     goldens/anti-goldens/metadata only; tighten tests (inject unknown fields,
     fixed golden snapshots, correct mutation descriptions); retain architecture
     and boundaries.
  8. Final validation. Full gate per-command (build/test/vet/gofmt-empty);
     non-modification audit over the extended protected set (locked sections
     byte-identical, before/after hashes); §7 checklist item-by-item; BLOCKED
     report if any math gate fails.

  Non-goals: new surveys, recon rewrites, new math/Go infrastructure,
  stochastic/backreaction/FLRW extensions, touching shared repos or the
  committed go.work.

  Validation Plan

  • Steps 1–4: each gate is a derived equation with its derivation shown
    (singular-coefficient match, smoothness proof, transport derivation,
    residual/correction/cancellation); reviewer can check each line against
    definitions.
  • Steps 5–6: errata appended (never silent edits — verified by lock-hash
    re-check over original scopes); 06/07/JSON agreement checked field-by-field
    (O-status table must match in all four places); Phys-Pseudo
    premise-completeness self-audit per PSEUDO §7.
  • Step 7: go build, go test -count=1, go vet exit 0, gofmt -l empty under the
    GOWORK/GOCACHE recipe; mutation tests kill wrong-coefficient and
    tampered-byte mutants; manifest/sidecar tests assert the tightened
    properties. Highest-risk validation: the Step 2–3 reconstruction itself — a
    formally clean derivation on wrongly normalized objects is exactly the
    failure being repaired, so Step 1's singularity match is the load-bearing
    check everything else stands on.

  Risks / Open Questions

  • Reconstruction may fail on its own terms (residual not a pure gradient,
    correction non-local) — then BLOCKED per prompt5, which is a valid outcome,
    not a plan failure.
  • L2-closure depth: bounded to stating and deriving the required transport
    equations plus order-of-vanishing, not a Hadamard-theory treatise.
  • Anchoring: the old coefficient must not leak in as a target — mitigated by
    derive-first ordering and by this plan stating no preview value.
  • C2 reversal changes a published audit verdict — mitigated by explicit
    erratum with before/after arithmetic, cross-referenced from 06/07.
  • External research: none — no new libraries or methods; DF text already
    inspected; Minkowski verification is analytic derivation.
  • Open questions for you: none — prompt5 is fully prescriptive.

  Plan to implement plan6_review.md (amended Plan 6: normalization-repair
  execution)

  Goal

  Execute prompt5's normalization repair under the four amendments and five
  execution tightenings of plan6_review.md, in Plan 6's existing order:
  distribution-level normalization audit, remainder/divergence reconstruction,
  neutral finding re-audit, provenance repair, fixture repair. QSG-T001 remains
  mathematically unpromoted until repaired work re-establishes it.

  Success criteria

  Amendment-driven acceptance (checked at the end, all must hold):

  1. A convention table exists with exact definitions and relations among G⁺,
     G⁻, G⁽¹⁾, H⁽¹⁾, and the Feynman object; no symbol silently changes meaning
     anywhere in the repair.
  2. The A-vs-B choice is recorded as a verified algebraic identity
     (whole-parametrix), not an expectation.
  3. No repaired document contains outcome-presuming language about prior
     findings (no "reversal anticipated", no "expected unaffected"); every
     S-dependent prior finding is marked provisional, re-derived, and documented
     with its dependency chain.
  4. The correction is presented with explicit uniqueness limits (minimal term
     vs conserved-addition freedom vs conditional restriction); the word
     "uniquely" appears nowhere unless its assumptions are established in the
     same document.
  5. O1's significance is decided and all three surfaces (06, 07, JSON) agree.
  6. The byte-identical set verifies byte-identical; the revised set contains
     only what this plan authorizes.
  7. The fixture's tightened suite states and enforces the
     integrity-vs-verification boundary (tests prove data integrity and replay,
     never physical correctness).
  8. All prompt5 §7 negative acceptance criteria still hold (no old-coefficient
     import, no fabricated S values, no silent overwrites, no scope drift, no
     GREEN certification).

  Constraints (fixed, from prompt5 + review)

  • Order: Plan 6's existing order (review's explicit recommendation).
  • Math gates: any failed derivation step → BLOCKED, documented, work stops for
    the gated chain.
  • Blind derivation: new remainder/KG/divergence work is derived fresh from DF
    definitions; DF is opened only at its Hadamard/parametrix normalization
    passages, no earlier.
  • The plan itself (and the review) establish nothing: the corrected
    prescription and every cancellation are results the repair must derive.

  Approach

  Single sequential executor (fresh-context cross-checker once, per §5). Eight
  steps, each with an evidence bar and a gate. Amendments are folded into the
  steps they govern (A1→Step 1, A2→Step 1, A3→Step 5, A4→Step 3; tightenings
  into Steps 2–7) rather than run as a separate pass.

  Step 1 — Distribution-level normalization audit (Amendments 1 + 2)

  Deliverable: an N1-family correction record (own subsection in 03's post-lock
  errata region or a new audit doc — decided at execution; append-only either
  way) containing:

  1. Convention table (exact, one row each): G⁺, G⁻, G⁽¹⁾, H⁽¹⁾, Feynman object
     — each with definition, normalization, distributional prescription,
     boundary values, and the relations among them. State which H-symbol is used
     at every citation; forbid silent H-meaning shifts.
  2. Sign and coefficient verification under the declared metric signature and σ
     convention — not just the leading 1/σ coefficient but the complete singular
     structure (U/σ and V ln terms), verified against DF's text at the pinned
     passages.
  3. Distributional prescriptions: Wightman boundary values, their symmetric
     combination, and the Feynman parametrix — which object each repaired claim
     concerns.
  4. Real-vs-complex scoping: which repaired statements concern the
     real/symmetric part and which concern the full complex distribution. Record
     explicitly: smooth-real-part-after-subtraction ≠
     smooth-full-complex-difference, until proven separately.
  5. A-vs-B algebraic resolution: state S_A = ½G⁽¹⁾ − H_A⁽¹⁾ and S_B = ½(G⁽¹⁾ −
     H_B⁽¹⁾); record S_A = S_B ⟺ H_B = 2H_A over the entire parametrix (leading,
     logarithmic, and all other normalization); then the prescription choice (A
     if DF confirms standard H⁽¹⁾) with its convention evidence. "Notationally
     equivalent" language is banned until this identity is written and verified.

  Gate: prescription frozen only when all five items hold; else BLOCKED.

  Step 2 — Remainder reconstruction (smoothness + J tightenings)

  1. State the Hadamard-state premise explicitly, then show the complete
     singular structure cancels under the frozen prescription (leading +
     logarithmic + smooth-remainder bookkeeping).
  2. Re-derive the Klein–Gordon equation from DF: state the exact differential
     operator, which argument it acts on, and the sign convention connecting the
     field equation to J. The old source coefficient is not imported, quoted as
     a target, or used as a checksum.
  3. Derive the transport equations and the expansion order needed for the
     point-split derivatives to have finite coincidence limits; the order claim
     must be a derived bound, not an asserted integer.
  4. Fresh-context cross-checker re-derives items 1–3 independently
     (derive-don't-receive: it is given the frozen conventions + DF passages,
     not the executor's derivation).

  Gate: executor and cross-checker agree on singular cancellation, KG form, and
  order bound; else reconcile or BLOCKED.

  Step 3 — Divergence reconstruction (Amendment 4)

  1. Recompute the divergence from the reconstructed remainder; derive the
     correction from the residual (never imported).
  2. Present the correction in three separated layers: (a) the minimal local
     metric-proportional conservation-restoring term; (b) the explicit freedom
     to add separately conserved local curvature terms; (c) the assumptions
     (existing A6 or newly stated ones) under which the renormalization
     ambiguity is restricted — and whether they are established.
  3. Layer-(a) cancellation is demonstrated by substitution; "uniquely
     determined" language is banned unless layer (c) establishes it.

  Gate: cancellation demonstrated; uniqueness claim either absent or
  assumption-backed.

  Step 4 — Source J + A-term reconciliation

  1. Reconcile the reconstructed KG equation with the A-term smoothness/limit
     treatment under the corrected definitions (or document precisely where they
     fail to reconcile).
  2. Any surviving inconsistency → BLOCKED with the contradiction stated.

  Step 5 — Neutral finding re-audit (Amendment 3)

  1. Provisional register: every prior Agent A and Agent B finding that depends
     directly or indirectly on the defective S is marked provisional. No finding
     is presumed to reverse or remain unchanged — including C2, C3–C7, and D–J.
  2. Four separate questions, answered separately with separate evidence (a
     positive answer to one establishes nothing about the others): (i) does
     Agent B's subtraction correctly remove the singularity; (ii) is the
     resulting real part smooth; (iii) is the full complex remainder smooth;
     (iv) does the remainder satisfy the claimed homogeneous/inhomogeneous field
     equation.
  3. Each re-derived claim is documented with its dependency chain back to the
     frozen prescription.
  4. O1 decision (tightening): the repair closes the new proof's O1 analog by
     construction — the Step 2 cross-checker derives the remainder rather than
     receiving it as a premise, so the remainder is independently derived in two
     contexts. The old O1 (against the voided proof) is retired as moot with an
     explicit note. O2/O5 semantics per Plan 6; all three metadata surfaces (§6)
     must agree.

  Step 6 — Provenance and metadata repair (errata-rules + O1–O5 tightenings)

  Byte-identical set (verify with sha256sum -c at start and end; current line
  counts for scope reference):

  • 01 (532 lines), 02 (538 lines) — fully frozen.
  • 03 lines 1–510 (Lock 1 scope; Lock header at 511), 04 lines 1–397 (Lock 2
    scope; Lock header at 398).
  • Recon conventions + Agent B PDF evidence file.

  Revised set (only these change, and only as stated):

  • 03/04/05: append-only errata + correction records in post-lock regions (05,
    380 lines, has no lock but stays append-only per "original documents
    intact").
  • 06 (213 lines): versioned revision — current bytes preserved verbatim as
    06-theorem-contract-v1-superseded.md; revised 06 restates theorem, gate
    answers, and the agreed O1–O5 semantics. No in-place silent rewrite.
  • 07 (141 lines): append-only superseding verdict section + explicit dated
    execution record fixing the chronology; original lines (including the stale
    Q9 line) stay visible with supersession notes.
  • manifest.json / research_metadata.json: revised in place (they are
    current-state metadata by nature); originals preserved as *.v1.json siblings
    that no test reads.

  06/07/JSON must agree on O1–O5 significance; the fixture manifest must match
  the audit's final theorem status.

  Step 7 — Fixture repair and test tightening (boundary tightening)

  1. Recalculate golden payloads, rational skeletons, and sidecar metadata from
     the repaired derivations (nothing carried over numerically).
  2. Tightening: fixed snapshots (not two-build comparison); unknown-field
     injection for strict decoding; mutation matrix re-run against the new
     goldens.
  3. Boundary statements (in test comments + manifest + AGENTS.md):
     snapshot/mutation tests prove the stored values changed-or-not (data
     integrity) and rational chains replay (arithmetic); they do not prove the
     physical coefficient is mathematically correct. Verification of QSG-T001
     remains the audit's job.

  Step 8 — Final validation and handoff

  Exact commands, run from the fixture directory
  (research/experiments/qsg-preflight-001/theorems/qsg-t001/), stated inline
  (not by reference):

    GOCACHE=/tmp/qsg-preflight/gocache GOWORK=$PWD/go.work GOPROXY=off go build
./...
    GOCACHE=/tmp/qsg-preflight/gocache GOWORK=$PWD/go.work GOPROXY=off go test -
count=1 -v ./...
    GOCACHE=/tmp/qsg-preflight/gocache GOWORK=$PWD/go.work GOPROXY=off go vet ./
...
    gofmt -l .

  Plus: sha256sum -c over the byte-identical set; git status --porcelain (only
  the authorized revised set dirty); prompt5 §7 checklist answered item by item;
  §8 ambiguity clauses re-checked post-repair.

  Validation

  • Per-step gates (§1–§4) with BLOCKED semantics; Steps 5–8 verified against
    the eight success criteria.
  • Non-modification audit: the byte-identical set verifies at both ends of the
    run.

  Risks

  • The distribution-level audit (Step 1) may surface further convention
    mismatches beyond the leading coefficient — that is its purpose;
    BLOCKED-with-evidence is a successful audit outcome, not a failure.
  • Neutral re-audit may strand findings other than C2 (C3–C7, D–J) as
    un-re-derivable — document each stranding with its dependency chain rather
    than forcing closure.
  • Ambiguity layers in Step 3 may leave the correction non-unique — report the
    ambiguity honestly; do not smuggle uniqueness through layer (c).

  go executes this plan.

   47 +
   48 +### N1.1 Minkowski verification of the Wightman singularity (DERIVED)
   49 +
   50 +Free massless field in Minkowski space, signature \((-+++)\).
   51 +\(G^+(x,x')=\int\!\frac{d^3k}{(2\pi)^3 2\omega}\,
   52 +e^{-i\omega\Delta t+i\mathbf k\cdot\Delta\mathbf x}\) with
   53 +\(\omega=|\mathbf k|\), \(\Delta t=t-t'\), \(r=|\Delta\mathbf x|\).
   54 +Spherical integration:
   55 +\[
   56 +G^+=\frac{1}{8\pi^2}\int_0^\infty\!dk\,k\,e^{-ik\Delta t}
   57 +\frac{e^{ikr}-e^{-ikr}}{ikr}
   58 +=\frac{1}{8\pi^2 ir}\int_0^\infty\!dk\,
   59 +\big[e^{-ik(\Delta t-r)}-e^{-ik(\Delta t+r)}\big].
   60 +\]
   61 +With \(\int_0^\infty dk\,e^{-ikb}=1/(ib)\) (\(\mathrm{Im}\,b<0\)):
   62 +\[
   63 +G^+=\frac{-i}{8\pi^2r}\cdot(-i)\cdot
   64 +\frac{2r}{(\Delta t-i\epsilon)^2-r^2}
   65 +=\frac{-1}{4\pi^2}\cdot\frac{1}{(\Delta t-i\epsilon)^2-r^2}.
   66 +\]
   67 +Since \((\Delta t-i\epsilon)^2-r^2=-s^2-2i\epsilon\Delta t\) with
   68 +\(s^2=-(\Delta t)^2+r^2=2\sigma\):
   69 +\[
   70 +\boxed{
   71 +G^+(x,x')=\frac{1}{8\pi^2}\cdot\frac{1}{\sigma+i\epsilon\Delta t}
   72 ++O(m^2,\ \text{regular}).
   73 +}
   74 +\tag{N1.1}
   75 +\]
   76 +For spacelike separation (\(\sigma>0\)) the \(i\epsilon\) is irrelevant:
   77 +\[
   78 +\boxed{G^+\sim\frac{1}{8\pi^2\sigma},\qquad
   79 +G^-\sim\frac{1}{8\pi^2\sigma}\quad\text{(spacelike)}.}
   80 +\tag{N1.2}
   81 +\]
   82 +\(G^-\) follows by \(x\leftrightarrow x'\) symmetry (\(\sigma\) symmetric
       ;
   83 +the \(i\epsilon\) sign flips, immaterial at \(\sigma>0\)). The mass term
   84 +adds \(O(m^2\ln|\sigma|)\)-type subleading corrections, not a leading-pol
       e
   85 +change. Disposition: DERIVED from the mode integral. The reviewer's
   86 +premise is independently confirmed; the sign is \(+\) (an earlier
   87 +scratch attempt dropped the angular-measure \(i\); recomputation fixes
   88 +it and the spacelike reality check \(G^+\in\mathbb R\) passes).
   89 +

  354 +### N3.2 Correction and cancellation (DERIVED)

  356 +(N3.1) is a pure gradient of a local scalar. With the ansatz
  357 +\(C_{\mu\nu}=K\,g_{\mu\nu}[v_1]\) (\(K\) constant):

  377 +DERIVED. The residual and the correction divergence are computed

  382 +### N3.3 Uniqueness limits (Amendment 4; binding language)
  383 +
  384 +A computed divergence does not by itself determine a tensor. The
  385 +repair distinguishes three layers and bans the word "uniquely"
  386 +unless layer (c) is established in the same document:
  387 +- (a) Minimal term: (N3.2) is the minimal local metric-proportional
  388 +conservation-restoring term — DERIVED (forced by (N3.1) given the
  389 +metric-proportional ansatz, which Wald 1978's abstract structure
  390 +"subtract that term times the metric" motivates and which the
  391 +gradient form of (N3.1) makes sufficient).
  392 +- (b) Conserved-addition freedom: any separately conserved local
  393 +curvature tensor may be added without changing the divergence.
  394 +Under the Wald axioms alone this freedom is the infinite
  395 +Tichy–Flanagan family (`01` §4.5, SOURCE-REPORTED) — DERIVED
  396 +(structural: metric variations of local invariants are conserved
  397 +by Theorem V).
  398 +- (c) Restriction assumptions: the polynomial dimension-four basis
  399 +(3.3) holds CONDITIONAL on Salcedo's renormalizability criterion
  400 +(A6, `01` §4.6); it is not derived from the Wald axioms.
  401 +The repaired theorem claims (a)+(b) with (c) conditional. The
  402 +coefficient \(3/(8\pi^2)\) is derived, not imported; it is the
  403 +coefficient of the minimal term, not a uniqueness claim.

  411 +### N3.5 Scale absorption re-run (DERIVED)
  412 +
  413 +\(\ell\to\ell'\): \(\Delta H^{(1)}=(1/8\pi^2)V\cdot(-2\ln(\ell'/\ell))\),
  414 +so \(\Delta S=-\Delta H^{(1)}=(1/8\pi^2)V\cdot2\ln(\ell'/\ell)\)
  415 +(no \(\tfrac12\): doubled vs the void §3.4, smooth symmetric
  416 +local-geometric). \(J_{\Delta S}=(\ln(\ell'/\ell)/4\pi^2)N_L\),
  417 +\([J_{\Delta S}]\equiv0\) ((V1a) everywhere by DP), hence
  418 +\(\nabla^\mu\lim T_{\mu\nu}\Delta S=\tfrac12\nabla_\nu[J_{\Delta S}]
  419 +=0\): \(\ell\)-changes shift the raw tensor by a conserved local
  420 +geometric tensor, absorbable into \(\Theta_{\mu\nu}\). Residual
  421 +(N3.1) and correction (N3.2) are \(\ell\)-independent. DERIVED.
  422 +
  423 +### N3.6 Trace re-run (DERIVED)
  424 +
  425 +From (1.1) with (N2.5): \(t=\tfrac12\Box s-m^2s-[J]\), so
  426 +\(\mathrm{tr}_{\rm raw}=-t-2m^2s
  427 +=-\tfrac12\Box s-m^2s+[J]\). With (N2.2) and
  428 +\(\mathrm{tr}\,C=4\cdot3[v_1]/(8\pi^2)\):
  429 +\[
  430 +\boxed{
  431 +\mathrm{tr}_{\rm ren}
  432 +=-\tfrac12\Box s-m^2s+\frac{3[v_1]}{4\pi^2}
  433 ++\Theta^\mu{}_\mu.
  434 +}
  435 +\tag{N3.4}
  436 +\]
  437 +Generically nonzero; compatible with (N3.3) (trace and divergence
  438 +are independent projections). DERIVED. Fixture-consumed rational
  439 +cores (hand-derived here, pinned in Step 7): TRACE
  440 +\(-3/4+3/2=+3/4\) (units \([v_1]/\pi^2\)); FLAT-CHAIN

├ \[gr-qc/0512118\] Hadamard renormalization of the stress-energy tensor for a q
│ uantized scalar field in a general spacetime of arbitrary dimension
│
│ # Hadamard renormalization of the stress-energy tensor
│ for a quantized scalar field in a general spacetime of arbitrary dimension
│
│  Yves Décanini Email: [decanini@univ-corse.fr](mailto:decanini@univ-corse.fr)
│ Affiliation: UMR CNRS 6134 SPE, Equipe Physique Semi-Classique (et) de la Mati
│ ère Condensée,
│ Université de Corse, Faculté des Sciences, BP 52, 20250 Corte, France    Antoi
│ ne Folacci Email: [folacci@univ-corse.fr](mailto:folacci@univ-corse.fr) Affili
│ ation: UMR CNRS 6134 SPE, Equipe Physique Semi-Classique (et) de la Matière Co
│ ndensée,
│ Université de Corse, Faculté des Sciences, BP 52, 20250 Corte, France
│
│ August 8, 2026
│
│ ###### Abstract
│
│ We develop the Hadamard renormalization of the stress-energy tensor for a mass
│ ive scalar field theory defined on a general spacetime of arbitrary dimension.
│  Our formalism could be helpful in treating some aspects of the quantum physic
│ s of extra spatial dimensions. More precisely, for spacetime dimension up to s
│ ix, we explicitly describe the Hadamard renormalization procedure and for spac
│ etime dimension from seven to eleven, we provide the framework permitting the
│ interested reader to perform this procedure explicitly in a given spacetime. W
│ e complete our study (i) by considering the ambiguities of the Hadamard renorm
│ alization of the stress-energy tensor and the corresponding ambiguities for th
│ e trace anomaly, (ii) by providing the expressions of the gravitational counte
│ rterms involved in the renormalization process (iii) by discussing the connect
│ ions between Hadamard renormalization and renormalization in the effective act
│ ion. All our results are expanded on standard bases for Riemann polynomials co
│ nstructed from group theoretical considerations and thus given on irreducible
│ forms.
│
│ ###### pacs
│
│ 04.62.+v, 11.10.Gh
│
│ ## I Introduction
│
│ In semiclassical gravity, spacetime is considered from a classical point of vi
│ ew, i.e. its metric gμ​νg\_{\\mu\\nu} is treated classically, while all the oth
│ er fields propagating on this background (from matter fields to the graviton f
│ ield at one-loop order) are assumed to be quantized. In the last thirty years,
│  this approximation of quantum gravity, usually called quantum field theory in
│  curved spacetime, has permitted us to obtain very interesting results concern
│ ing more particularly i) quantum black hole physics in connection with Hawking
│  radiation, ii) early universe cosmology, iii) the Casimir effect and iv) quan
│ tum violations of classical energy conditions in connection with both the sing
│ ularity theorems of Hawking and Penrose and the existence of traversable wormh
│ oles and time-machines… We refer to the monographs of Birrell and Davies [Birr
│ ell and Davies 1982](#bib.bib1 ""), Fulling [Fulling 1989](#bib.bib2 "") and W
│ ald [Wald 1994](#bib.bib3 "") as well as to references therein for various asp
│ ects of semiclassical gravity. We also refer to a recent review by Ford [Ford
│ arXiv:gr-qc/0504096](#bib.bib4 "") which is a short but rather up to date intr
│ oduction to semiclassical gravity and to its applications. We finally refer to
│  Sec. II.B of Ref. [Flanagan and Wald 1996](#bib.bib5 "") for a very interesti
│ ng critical account about the status and the domain of applicability of semicl
│ assical gravity and to Refs. [Hu and Verdaguer 2001](#bib.bib6 ""); [Hu and Ve
│ rdaguer 2004](#bib.bib7 "") for an extension of semiclassical gravity, the so-
│ called semiclassical stochastic gravity, which also permits us to discuss and
│ investigate its validity.
│
│ For a quantum field in some normalized state |ψ⟩|\\psi\\rangle, the expectatio
│ n value with respect to |ψ⟩|\\psi\\rangle of its associated stress-energy-tens
│ or operator Tμ​νT\_{\\mu\\nu}, denoted ⟨ψ|Tμ​ν|ψ⟩\\langle\\psi|T\_{\\mu\\nu}|\\p
│ si\\rangle, plays a central role in semiclassical gravity. Indeed:
│
│ – In curved spacetime, the particle concept is in general very nebulous. Here,
│  we adhere completely to the point of view developed by Davies in Ref. [Davies
│  1984](#bib.bi
│
