│ func rat(n, d int64) physmath.Expr { return physmath.MustRational(n, d) }
│
│ // Build constructs the equivariance evidence chain. It panics on programmer
│ // error; replay/validation failures of crafted artifacts surface through
│ // Verify in protocol tests.
│ func Build() *Fixture {
│     s := physmath.NewMemStore()
│     f := &Fixture{Store: s, Symbols: map[string]artifact.ArtifactRef{}, Conds:
│  map[string]artifact.ArtifactRef{}}
│     pt := physmath.DeriveParameterID(0)
│     pq := physmath.DeriveParameterID(1)
│     t := physmath.NewBoundSymbol("t", pt)
│     q := physmath.NewBoundSymbol("q", pq)
│     pids := []physmath.ParameterID{pt, pq}
│     names := []string{"t", "q"}
│
│     // 1D line chart for coordinates ["q"].
│     qExprRef := putExpr(s, q)
│     chart := physmath.CoordinateSystem{Dimension: 1, CoordinateRefs:
│ []artifact.ArtifactRef{qExprRef}, SpaceRef: spaceCollection(s, "Q1")}
│     if err := chart.Validate(); err != nil {
│         panic(err)
│     }
│     chartRef, err := s.Put(physmath.FamilyCoordinateSystem, chart.Encode())
│     if err != nil {
│         panic(err)
│     }
│     f.ChartRef = chartRef
│
│     // Solution pair: R1 = 1, S1 = 2q.
│     r1fn := mintFn(s, pids, names, rat(1, 1))
│     f.R1FnRef = r1fn
│     f.R1Ref, err = refOf(s, r1fn)
│     if err != nil {
│         panic(err)
│     }
│     s1fn := mintFn(s, pids, names, physmath.Mul(rat(2, 1), q))
│     f.S1FnRef = s1fn
│     f.S1Ref, err = refOf(s, s1fn)
│     if err != nil {
│         panic(err)
│     }
│     rho1, rho1Deriv := simpReplay(s, putExpr(s, physmath.Mul(mustResolve(s,
│ f.R1Ref), mustResolve(s, f.R1Ref))), nil)
│     f.Rho1Ref = rho1
│     f.Derivs = append(f.Derivs, rho1Deriv)
│     dtRho1, dtRho1Deriv := diffReplay(s, f.Rho1Ref, r1fn, pt, 1, nil)
│     f.DtRho1Ref = dtRho1
│     dqS1, dqS1Deriv := diffReplay(s, f.S1Ref, s1fn, pq, 1, nil)
│     f.DqS1Ref = dqS1
│     j1, j1Deriv := simpReplay(s, putExpr(s, physmath.Mul(mustResolve(s,
│ f.Rho1Ref), mustResolve(s, f.DqS1Ref))), nil)
│     f.J1Ref = j1
│     dqJ1, dqJ1Deriv := diffReplay(s, f.J1Ref, r1fn, pq, 1, nil)
│     f.DqJ1Ref = dqJ1
│     res1, res1Deriv := simpReplay(s, putExpr(s, physmath.Add(mustResolve(s,
│ f.DtRho1Ref), mustResolve(s, f.DqJ1Ref))), nil)
│     f.Res1Ref = res1
│     f.Derivs = append(f.Derivs, dtRho1Deriv, dqS1Deriv, j1Deriv, dqJ1Deriv,
│ res1Deriv)
│     f.Res1ZeroRef = putRelation(s, physmath.OpEQ, mustResolve(s, f.Res1Ref),
│ rat(0, 1))
│     v1, v1Deriv := simpReplay(s, putExpr(s, physmath.Mul(mustResolve(s,
│ f.J1Ref), physmath.Pow(mustResolve(s, f.Rho1Ref), rat(-1, 1)))), nil)
│     f.V1Ref = v1
│     f.Derivs = append(f.Derivs, v1Deriv)
│     f.Guide1RelRef = putRelation(s, physmath.OpEQ, mustResolve(s, f.V1Ref),
│         physmath.Mul(mustResolve(s, f.J1Ref), physmath.Pow(mustResolve(s,
│ f.Rho1Ref), rat(-1, 1))))
│     f.Conds["rho1Nonzero"] = condNEQ(s, mustResolve(s, f.Rho1Ref))
│
│     // Non-solution pair: R2 = t^2+q^2+1, S2 = t q.
│     r2body := physmath.Add(physmath.Pow(t, rat(2, 1)), physmath.Pow(q, rat(2,
│ 1)), rat(1, 1))
│     r2fn := mintFn(s, pids, names, r2body)
│     f.R2FnRef = r2fn
│     f.R2Ref, err = refOf(s, r2fn)
│     if err != nil {
│         panic(err)
│     }
│     s2fn := mintFn(s, pids, names, physmath.Mul(t, q))
│     f.S2FnRef = s2fn
│     f.S2Ref, err = refOf(s, s2fn)
│     if err != nil {
│         panic(err)
│     }
│     rho2, rho2Deriv := simpReplay(s, putExpr(s, physmath.Mul(mustResolve(s,
│ f.R2Ref), mustResolve(s, f.R2Ref))), nil)
│     f.Rho2Ref = rho2
│     f.Symbols["rho2"] = rho2
│     dtRho2, dtRho2Deriv := diffReplay(s, f.Rho2Ref, r2fn, pt, 1, nil)
│     f.DtRho2Ref = dtRho2
│     dqS2, dqS2Deriv := diffReplay(s, f.S2Ref, s2fn, pq, 1, nil)
│     f.DqS2Ref = dqS2
│     j2, j2Deriv := simpReplay(s, putExpr(s, physmath.Mul(mustResolve(s,
│ f.Rho2Ref), mustResolve(s, f.DqS2Ref))), nil)
│     f.J2Ref = j2
│     f.Symbols["j2"] = j2
│     dqJ2, dqJ2Deriv := diffReplay(s, f.J2Ref, r2fn, pq, 1, nil)
│     f.DqJ2Ref = dqJ2
│     res2, res2Deriv := simpReplay(s, putExpr(s, physmath.Add(mustResolve(s,
│ f.DtRho2Ref), mustResolve(s, f.DqJ2Ref))), nil)
│     f.Res2Ref = res2
│     f.Derivs = append(f.Derivs, rho2Deriv, dtRho2Deriv, dqS2Deriv, j2Deriv,
│ dqJ2Deriv, res2Deriv)
│     // Manual product-rule assembly of d_q j2 = d_q(rho2 d_q S2).
│     dqR2, dqR2Deriv := diffReplay(s, f.R2Ref, r2fn, pq, 1, nil)
│     f.Derivs = append(f.Derivs, dqR2Deriv)
│     d2qS2, d2qS2Deriv := diffReplay(s, f.DqS2Ref, s2fn, pq, 1, nil)
│     f.Derivs = append(f.Derivs, d2qS2Deriv)
│     manual := physmath.Add(
│         physmath.Mul(rat(2, 1), mustResolve(s, f.R2Ref), mustResolve(s, dqR2),
│  mustResolve(s, f.DqS2Ref)),
│         physmath.Mul(mustResolve(s, f.Rho2Ref), mustResolve(s, d2qS2)))
│     manualRef, manualDeriv := simpReplay(s, putExpr(s, manual), nil)
│     f.Derivs = append(f.Derivs, manualDeriv)
│     f.PRRelRef = putRelation(s, physmath.OpEQ, mustResolve(s, f.DqJ2Ref),
│ mustResolve(s, manualRef))
│     v2, v2Deriv := simpReplay(s, putExpr(s, physmath.Mul(mustResolve(s,
│ f.J2Ref), physmath.Pow(mustResolve(s, f.Rho2Ref), rat(-1, 1)))), nil)
│     f.V2Ref = v2
│     f.Derivs = append(f.Derivs, v2Deriv)
│     f.Guide2RelRef = putRelation(s, physmath.OpEQ, mustResolve(s, f.V2Ref),
│         physmath.Mul(mustResolve(s, f.J2Ref), physmath.Pow(mustResolve(s,
│ f.Rho2Ref), rat(-1, 1))))
│     f.Conds["rho2Nonzero"] = condNEQ(s, mustResolve(s, f.Rho2Ref))
│
│     // Node pair: R3 = q, S3 = q (rho3 = q^2 vanishes at q = 0).
│     r3fn := mintFn(s, pids, names, q)
│     f.R3FnRef = r3fn
│     f.R3Ref, err = refOf(s, r3fn)
│     if err != nil {
│         panic(err)
│     }
│     s3fn := mintFn(s, pids, names, q)
│     f.S3FnRef = s3fn
│     f.S3Ref, err = refOf(s, s3fn)
│     if err != nil {
│         panic(err)
│     }
│     rho3, rho3Deriv := simpReplay(s, putExpr(s, physmath.Mul(mustResolve(s,
│ f.R3Ref), mustResolve(s, f.R3Ref))), nil)
│     f.Rho3Ref = rho3
│     dqS3, dqS3Deriv := diffReplay(s, f.S3Ref, s3fn, pq, 1, nil)
│     j3, j3Deriv := simpReplay(s, putExpr(s, physmath.Mul(mustResolve(s,
│ f.Rho3Ref), mustResolve(s, dqS3))), nil)
│     f.J3Ref = j3
│     v3, v3Deriv := simpReplay(s, putExpr(s, physmath.Mul(mustResolve(s,
│ f.J3Ref), physmath.Pow(mustResolve(s, f.Rho3Ref), rat(-1, 1)))), nil)
│     f.V3Ref = v3
│     f.Derivs = append(f.Derivs, rho3Deriv, dqS3Deriv, j3Deriv, v3Deriv)
│     f.Guide3RelRef = putRelation(s, physmath.OpEQ, mustResolve(s, f.V3Ref),
│         physmath.Mul(mustResolve(s, f.J3Ref), physmath.Pow(mustResolve(s,
│ f.Rho3Ref), rat(-1, 1))))
│     f.Conds["rho3Nonzero"] = condNEQ(s, mustResolve(s, f.Rho3Ref))
│
│     // Represented-only claim relations over opaque symbols: flow ODE,
│     // pushforward transport, and the (E-loc) equivariance claim. No
│     // derivation targets them (enforced by protocol tests); they record
│     // what the fixture does not execute.
│     dQdt := physmath.NewEphemeralSymbol("dQdt")
│     vFlow := physmath.NewEphemeralSymbol("vFlow")
│     f.FlowRelRef = putRelation(s, physmath.OpEQ, dQdt, vFlow)
│     pushLHS := physmath.NewEphemeralSymbol("pushRho0")
│     rhoT := physmath.NewEphemeralSymbol("rhoT")
│     f.PushRelRef = putRelation(s, physmath.OpEQ, pushLHS, rhoT)
│     f.EquivRelRef = putRelation(s, physmath.OpEQ, pushLHS, rhoT)
│     return f
│ }
│
│ // Verify replays every stored derivation and checks the binding of each
│ // guidance relation to its quotient construction and NEQ condition, plus
│ // the residual-zero and product-rule relation contents.
│ func (f *Fixture) Verify() error {
│     for i, dref := range f.Derivs {
│         if err := replayDerivation(f.Store, i, dref); err != nil {
│             return err
│         }
│     }
│     if err := checkGuideBinding(f.Store, f.Guide1RelRef, f.V1Ref, f.J1Ref,
│ f.Rho1Ref, f.Conds["rho1Nonzero"], "pair1"); err != nil {
│         return err
│     }
│     if err := checkGuideBinding(f.Store, f.Guide2RelRef, f.V2Ref, f.J2Ref,
│ f.Rho2Ref, f.Conds["rho2Nonzero"], "pair2"); err != nil {
│         return err
│     }
│     if err := checkGuideBinding(f.Store, f.Guide3RelRef, f.V3Ref, f.J3Ref,
│ f.Rho3Ref, f.Conds["rho3Nonzero"], "pair3"); err != nil {
│         return err
│     }
│     if err := checkResidualZero(f.Store, f.Res1ZeroRef, f.Res1Ref); err != nil
│  {
│         return err
│     }
│     return checkPRRelation(f.Store, f.PRRelRef, f.DqJ2Ref)
│ }
│
│ func replayDerivation(s physmath.Store, i int, dref artifact.ArtifactRef)
│ error {
│     fam, payload, err := s.Lookup(dref)
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("derivation %d unresolvable: %w", i, err)
│     }
│     if fam != physmath.FamilyDerivation {
│         return fmt.Errorf("derivation %d is not derivation/1", i)
│     }
│     d, err := physmath.DecodeDerivation(payload)
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("derivation %d undecodable: %w", i, err)
│     }
│     if err := physmath.Replay(s, d, nil); err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("derivation %d does not replay: %w", i, err)
│     }
│     return nil
│ }
│
│ // checkGuideBinding verifies a guidance relation is EQ(v, j rho^-1) with
│ // v the stored guidance artifact and quotient sides matching the stored
│ // current/density, and that the binding NEQ condition exists with body
│ // NEQ(rho, 0). A node-blind assembly (same quotient, no condition)
│ // fails this check.
│ func checkGuideBinding(s physmath.Store, relRef, vRef, jRef, rhoRef, condRef
│ artifact.ArtifactRef, tag string) error {
│     op, left, right, err := decodeRelationPayload(s, relRef)
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("%s guidance relation: %w", tag, err)
│     }
│     if op != string(physmath.OpEQ) {
│         return fmt.Errorf("%s guidance relation is not an equality", tag)
│     }
│     vb, err := exprBytes(s, vRef)
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("%s guidance v: %w", tag, err)
│     }
│     lb, err := left.Encode()
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("%s guidance left unencodable: %w", tag, err)
│     }
│     if string(lb) != string(vb) {
│         return fmt.Errorf("%s guidance left side is not the stored v", tag)
│     }
│     want, err := physmath.Canonicalize(physmath.Mul(mustResolve(s, jRef),
│         physmath.Pow(mustResolve(s, rhoRef), rat(-1, 1))))
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("%s guidance expectation unbuildable: %w", tag, err)
│     }
│     got, err := physmath.Canonicalize(right)
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("%s guidance right uncanonicalizable: %w", tag, err)
│     }
│     if !got.Equal(want) {
│         return fmt.Errorf("%s guidance right side is not j rho^-1", tag)
│     }
│     cop, cleft, cright, err := decodeCondition(s, condRef)
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("%s guidance condition: %w", tag, err)
│     }
│     if cop != string(physmath.OpNEQ) {
│         return fmt.Errorf("%s guidance condition is not NEQ", tag)
│     }
│     rhob, err := exprBytes(s, rhoRef)
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("%s guidance rho: %w", tag, err)
│     }
│     clb, err := cleft.Encode()
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("%s condition left unencodable: %w", tag, err)
│     }
│     if string(clb) != string(rhob) {
│         return fmt.Errorf("%s condition does not mention stored rho", tag)
│     }
│     zero, err := rat(0, 1).Encode()
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("%s zero unencodable: %w", tag, err)
│     }
│     crb, err := cright.Encode()
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("%s condition right unencodable: %w", tag, err)
│     }
│     if string(crb) != string(zero) {
│         return fmt.Errorf("%s condition is not NEQ(rho, 0)", tag)
│     }
│     return nil
│ }
│
│ // checkResidualZero verifies the solution-residual relation is EQ with the
│ // stored residual on the left and canonical zero on the right.
│ func checkResidualZero(s physmath.Store, relRef, storedRef
│ artifact.ArtifactRef) error {
│     op, left, right, err := decodeRelationPayload(s, relRef)
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("solution residual relation: %w", err)
│     }
│     if op != string(physmath.OpEQ) {
│         return fmt.Errorf("solution residual relation is not an equality")
│     }
│     sb, err := exprBytes(s, storedRef)
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("solution residual stored: %w", err)
│     }
│     lb, err := left.Encode()
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("solution residual left unencodable: %w", err)
│     }
│     if string(lb) != string(sb) {
│         return fmt.Errorf("solution residual left side is not the stored
│ artifact")
│     }
│     got, err := physmath.Canonicalize(right)
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("solution residual right uncanonicalizable: %w",
│ err)
│     }
│     want, err := physmath.Canonicalize(rat(0, 1))
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("solution residual zero uncanonicalizable: %w", err)
│     }
│     if !got.Equal(want) {
│         return fmt.Errorf("solution residual right side is not zero")
│     }
│     return nil
│ }
│
│ // checkPRRelation verifies the product-rule relation structurally: EQ with
│ // the stored executed divergence on the left and a resolvable expression
│ // on the right. The identity itself (executed vs manual assembly) is
│ // asserted by protocol tests with hand-verified commentary, not here.
│ func checkPRRelation(s physmath.Store, relRef, storedRef artifact.ArtifactRef)
│  error {
│     op, left, _, err := decodeRelationPayload(s, relRef)
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("product-rule relation: %w", err)
│     }
│     if op != string(physmath.OpEQ) {
│         return fmt.Errorf("product-rule relation is not an equality")
│     }
│     sb, err := exprBytes(s, storedRef)
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("product-rule stored: %w", err)
│     }
│     lb, err := left.Encode()
│     if err != nil {
│         return fmt.Errorf("product-rule left unencodable: %w", err)
│     }
│     if string(lb) != string(sb) {
│         return fmt.Errorf("product-rule left side is not the stored
│ divergence")
│     }
│     return nil
│ }
│
│ func decodeRelationPayload(s physmath.Store, relRef artifact.ArtifactRef)
│ (string, physmath.Expr, physmath.Expr, error) {
│     fam, payload, err := s.Lookup(relRef)
│     if err != nil {
│         return "", nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("unresolvable: %w", err)
│     }
│     if fam != physmath.FamilyRelation {
│         return "", nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("not relation/1")
│     }
│     return decodeRelationBody(payload)
│ }
│
│ func decodeRelationBody(payload []byte) (string, physmath.Expr, physmath.Expr,
│  error) {
│     rd := artifact.NewDecoder(payload)
│     opStr, err := rd.String()
│     if err != nil {
│         return "", nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("missing operator: %w", err)
│     }
│     left, err := physmath.DecodeExpr(rd)
│     if err != nil {
│         return "", nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("left undecodable: %w", err)
│     }
│     right, err := physmath.DecodeExpr(rd)
│     if err != nil {
│         return "", nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("right undecodable: %w", err)
│     }
│     if !rd.Exhausted() {
│         return "", nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("trailing bytes")
│     }
│     return opStr, left, right, nil
│ }
│
│ func decodeCondition(s physmath.Store, condRef artifact.ArtifactRef) (string,
│ physmath.Expr, physmath.Expr, error) {
│     fam, payload, err := s.Lookup(condRef)
│     if err != nil {
│         return "", nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("unresolvable: %w", err)
│     }
│     if fam != physmath.FamilyCondition {
│         return "", nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("not condition/1")
│     }
│     relRef, err := artifact.DecodeArtifactRef(artifact.NewDecoder(payload))
│     if err != nil {
│         return "", nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("condition does not wrap a ref: %w",
│ err)
│     }
│     return decodeRelationPayload(s, relRef)
│ }
│
│ func exprBytes(s physmath.Store, ref artifact.ArtifactRef) ([]byte, error) {
│     fam, payload, err := s.Lookup(ref)
│     if err != nil {
│         return nil, err
│     }
│     if fam != physmath.FamilyExpression {
│         return nil, fmt.Errorf("not expression/1")
│     }
│     return payload, nil
│ }
│ GOEOF
│ echo FIXTURE-WRITTEN; wc -l
│ /home/chaschel/Documents/go/phys-lib/bohm/equivariance/fixture.go
├ exit 0
└ FIXTURE-WRITTEN

◆ Ran command · Write tests, build fixture, run probe · ✗ · 23s
├ export GOCACHE=/tmp/bohm-equiv-gocache &&
│ EQ=/home/chaschel/Documents/go/phys-lib/bohm/equivariance && cat >
│ $EQ/manifest_test.go <<'GOEOF'
│ // Package equivariance sidecar tests: strict structural validation of
│ // source.json and source_mapping.json plus manifest limitation pins.
│ // These tests validate linkage structure only, never semantic equivalence.
│ package equivariance
│
│ import (
│     "bytes"
│     "encoding/json"
│     "os"
│     "path/filepath"
│     "reflect"
│     "runtime"
│     "strings"
│     "testing"
│
│     "github.com/PithomLabs/phys-artifact"
│ )
│
│ type mappingEntry struct {
│     SourceEq          string   `json:"source_eq"`
│     SourceQuantity    string   `json:"source_quantity"`
│     Meaning           string   `json:"meaning"`
│     DefiningRelation  string   `json:"defining_relation"`
│     FixtureRef        string   `json:"fixture_ref"`
│     Dependencies      []string `json:"dependencies"`
│     AllowUnrepresentd bool     `json:"allow_unrepresented"`
│ }
│
│ type mappingDoc struct {
│     SourceMapping []mappingEntry `json:"source_mapping"`
│ }
│
│ type sourceDoc struct {
│     Source struct {
│         ArxivID        string `json:"arxiv_id"`
│         JournalRef     string `json:"journal_ref"`
│         ScopeNote      string `json:"scope_note"`
│         Representation string `json:"representation"`
│     } `json:"source"`
│ }
│
│ type manifestLimits struct {
│     Limitations []struct {
│         ID        string `json:"id"`
│         Statement string `json:"statement"`
│     } `json:"limitations"`
│ }
│
│ func sidecarPath(t *testing.T, name string) string {
│     t.Helper()
│     _, thisFile, _, ok := runtime.Caller(0)
│     if !ok {
│         t.Fatal("runtime.Caller failed")
│     }
│     return filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(thisFile), name)
│ }
│
│ func loadMapping(t *testing.T) mappingDoc {
│     t.Helper()
│     raw, err := os.ReadFile(sidecarPath(t, "source_mapping.json"))
│     if err != nil {
│         t.Fatal(err)
│     }
│     var doc mappingDoc
│     dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(raw))
│     dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
│     if err := dec.Decode(&doc); err != nil {
│         t.Fatalf("source_mapping strict decode: %v", err)
│     }
│     return doc
│ }
│
│ func resolveFixtureRef(f *Fixture, name string) bool {
│     name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
│     v := reflect.ValueOf(f).Elem()
│     if fv := v.FieldByName(name); fv.IsValid() && fv.Type() ==
│ reflect.TypeOf(artifact.ArtifactRef{}) {
│         return true
│     }
│     if _, ok := f.Conds[name]; ok {
│         return true
│     }
│     if _, ok := f.Symbols[name]; ok {
│         return true
│     }
│     return false
│ }
│
│ func inScope(eq string) bool {
│     for _, p := range []string{"01:", "02:", "03:", "fixture:"} {
│         if strings.HasPrefix(eq, p) && len(eq) > len(p) {
│             return true
│         }
│     }
│     return false
│ }
│
│ func validateMapping(f *Fixture, doc mappingDoc) error {
│     t := &testing.T{}
│     _ = t
│     for i, e := range doc.SourceMapping {
│         if e.SourceEq == "" || e.SourceQuantity == "" || e.Meaning == "" ||
│ e.DefiningRelation == "" || e.FixtureRef == "" {
│             return errf("entry %d missing required field", i)
│         }
│         if !inScope(e.SourceEq) {
│             return errf("entry %d source_eq %q out of scope", i, e.SourceEq)
│         }
│         for _, r := range strings.Split(e.FixtureRef, ",") {
│             if !resolveFixtureRef(f, r) {
│                 return errf("entry %d fixture_ref %q unresolvable", i, r)
│             }
│         }
│         for _, d := range e.Dependencies {
│             if !resolveFixtureRef(f, d) {
│                 return errf("entry %d dependency %q unresolvable", i, d)
│             }
│         }
│     }
│     return nil
│ }
│
│ type terr string
│
│ func (e terr) Error() string { return string(e) }
│
│ func errf(format string, args ...any) error { return terr(sprintf(format,
│ args...)) }
│
│ func sprintf(format string, args ...any) string {
│     // Minimal formatting without importing fmt into test helpers twice.
│     out := format
│     for _, a := range args {
│         out = strings.Replace(out, "%d", itoa(a), 1)
│         out = strings.Replace(out, "%q", quote(a), 1)
│     }
│     return out
│ }
│
│ func itoa(a any) string {
│     switch v := a.(type) {
│     case int:
│         if v == 0 {
│             return "0"
│         }
│         neg := v < 0
│         if neg {
│             v = -v
│         }
│         digits := ""
│         for v > 0 {
│             digits = string(rune('0'+v%10)) + digits
│             v /= 10
│         }
│         if neg {
│             return "-" + digits
│         }
│         return digits
│     default:
│         return "?"
│     }
│ }
│
│ func quote(a any) string {
│     if s, ok := a.(string); ok {
│         return "\"" + s + "\""
│     }
│     return "?"
│ }
│
│ func TestSourceMappingStructuralConsistency(t *testing.T) {
│     f := Build()
│     if err := validateMapping(f, loadMapping(t)); err != nil {
│         t.Fatal(err)
│     }
│ }
│
│ func TestSourceMappingRejectsCorruptions(t *testing.T) {
│     f := Build()
│     doc := loadMapping(t)
│     mut := doc
│     mut.SourceMapping[0].FixtureRef = "NoSuchRef"
│     if err := validateMapping(f, mut); err == nil {
│         t.Fatal("accepted unresolvable fixture_ref")
│     }
│     mut = doc
│     mut.SourceMapping[1].SourceEq = "99:bogus"
│     if err := validateMapping(f, mut); err == nil {
│         t.Fatal("accepted out-of-scope source_eq")
│     }
│     mut = doc
│     mut.SourceMapping[2].Meaning = ""
│     if err := validateMapping(f, mut); err == nil {
│         t.Fatal("accepted entry with missing field")
│     }
│ }
│
│ func TestSourceSidecar(t *testing.T) {
│     raw, err := os.ReadFile(sidecarPath(t, "source.json"))
│     if err != nil {
│         t.Fatal(err)
│     }
│     var doc sourceDoc
│     dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(raw))
│     dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
│     if err := dec.Decode(&doc); err != nil {
│         t.Fatalf("source strict decode: %v", err)
│     }
│     if doc.Source.ArxivID == "" || doc.Source.ScopeNote == "" ||
│ doc.Source.Representation == "" {
│         t.Fatal("source sidecar missing required identity field")
│     }
│ }
│
│ func TestManifestLimitations(t *testing.T) {
│     raw, err := os.ReadFile(sidecarPath(t, "manifest.json"))
│     if err != nil {
│         t.Fatal(err)
│     }
│     var m manifestLimits
│     if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &m); err != nil {
│         t.Fatal(err)
│     }
│     have := map[string]bool{}
│     for _, l := range m.Limitations {
│         have[l.ID] = true
│     }
│     for _, id := range []string{"no_flow_execution", "no_pushforward",
│ "no_uniqueness_pde", "no_global_existence", "no_node_behavior",
│ "no_schrodinger_residual", "test_units_only", "fragment_1d"} {
│         if !have[id] {
│             t.Fatalf("manifest limitations missing %s", id)
│         }
│     }
│ }

   20 +func mustResolve(t *testing.T, s physmath.Store, ref artifact.ArtifactRef
       ) physmath.Expr {
   21 +    t.Helper()
   22 +    fam, payload, err := s.Lookup(ref)
   23 +    if err != nil {
   24 +        t.Fatal(err)
   25 +    }
   26 +    if fam != physmath.FamilyExpression {
   27 +        t.Fatalf("ref is %s, want expression/1", fam)
   28 +    }
   29 +    e, err := physmath.DecodeExpression(payload)
   30 +    if err != nil {
   31 +        t.Fatal(err)
   32 +    }
   33 +    return e
   34 +}
   35 +
   36 +func canon(t *testing.T, e physmath.Expr) physmath.Expr {
   37 +    t.Helper()
   38 +    c, err := physmath.Canonicalize(e)
   39 +    if err != nil {
   40 +        t.Fatal(err)
   41 +    }
   42 +    return c
   43 +}

   45 +func assertCanonEq(t *testing.T, got, want physmath.Expr, msg string) {
   46 +    t.Helper()
   47 +    if !canon(t, got).Equal(canon(t, want)) {
   48 +        t.Fatalf("%s: canonical forms differ", msg)
   49 +    }
   50 +}
   51 +
   52 +func assertCanonNe(t *testing.T, a, b physmath.Expr, msg string) {
   53 +    t.Helper()
   54 +    if canon(t, a).Equal(canon(t, b)) {
   55 +        t.Fatalf("%s: canonical forms unexpectedly equal", msg)
   56 +    }
   57 +}

   61 +// boundSyms mirrors the fixture's (name, pid) scheme: t=pid0, q=pid1.
   62 +func boundSyms() (t, q physmath.Expr) {
   63 +    pt := physmath.DeriveParameterID(0)
   64 +    pq := physmath.DeriveParameterID(1)
   65 +    return physmath.NewBoundSymbol("t", pt), physmath.NewBoundSymbol("q",
        pq)
   66 +}

   68 +func diffParams(fn artifact.ArtifactRef, pid physmath.ParameterID, order
       uint32) []byte {
   69 +    return append(physmath.BoundParameterRef{DeclaringArtifactRef: fn, Pa
       rameterID: pid}.Encode(),
   70 +        artifact.EncodeUInt32(order)...)
   71 +}
   72 +
   73 +// decodeRelationBody mirrors the fixture's relation decoder:
   74 +// Enum(operator) || left || right.
   75 +func decodeRelationBody(t *testing.T, payload []byte) (string, physmath.E
       xpr, physmath.Expr) {
   76 +    t.Helper()
   77 +    rd := artifact.NewDecoder(payload)
   78 +    opStr, err := rd.String()
   79 +    if err != nil {
   80 +        t.Fatal(err)
   81 +    }
   82 +    left, err := physmath.DecodeExpr(rd)
   83 +    if err != nil {
   84 +        t.Fatal(err)
   85 +    }
   86 +    right, err := physmath.DecodeExpr(rd)
   87 +    if err != nil {
   88 +        t.Fatal(err)
   89 +    }
   90 +    if !rd.Exhausted() {
   91 +        t.Fatal("relation body has trailing bytes")
   92 +    }
   93 +    return opStr, left, right
   94 +}
   95 +
   96 +func applySimplify(t *testing.T, s physmath.Store, target artifact.Artifa
       ctRef) artifact.ArtifactRef {
   97 +    t.Helper()
   98 +    out, err := physmath.Apply(s, physmath.Invocation{
   99 +        ProducerNamespace: physmath.NamespacePhysMath, RuleID: physmath.R
       uleExprSimplify,
  100 +        InputRefs: []artifact.ArtifactRef{target},
  101 +    })
  102 +    if err != nil {
  103 +        t.Fatal(err)
  104 +    }
  105 +    return out.OutputRef
  106 +}
  107 +
  108 +func putExpr(t *testing.T, s physmath.Store, e physmath.Expr) artifact.Ar
       tifactRef {
  109 +    t.Helper()
  110 +    ref, err := physmath.PutExpression(s, e)
  111 +    if err != nil {
  112 +        t.Fatal(err)
  113 +    }
  114 +    return ref
  115 +}

  117 +// TestVerifyAcceptsFresh pins the end-to-end gate: Build then Verify.
  118 +func TestVerifyAcceptsFresh(t *testing.T) {
  119 +    if err := ms.Verify(ms.Build()); err != nil {
  120 +        t.Fatalf("fresh fixture rejected: %v", err)
  121 +    }
  122 +}
  123 +
  124 +// TestDerivationInventory pins the full evidence count (30 single-step
  125 +// derivations) and replays every one.
  126 +func TestDerivationInventory(t *testing.T) {
  127 +    f := ms.Build()
  128 +    if len(f.Derivs) != 30 {
  129 +        t.Fatalf("derivation count = %d, want 30", len(f.Derivs))
  130 +    }
  131 +    for i, dref := range f.Derivs {
  132 +        fam, payload, err := f.Store.Lookup(dref)
  133 +        if err != nil {
  134 +            t.Fatalf("deriv %d unresolvable: %v", i, err)
  135 +        }
  136 +        if fam != physmath.FamilyDerivation {
  137 +            t.Fatalf("deriv %d family = %s", i, fam)
  138 +        }
  139 +        d, err := physmath.DecodeDerivation(payload)
  140 +        if err != nil {
  141 +            t.Fatalf("deriv %d undecodable: %v", i, err)
  142 +        }
  143 +        if err := d.Validate(); err != nil {
  144 +            t.Fatalf("deriv %d invalid: %v", i, err)
  145 +        }
  146 +        if len(d.Steps) != 1 {
  147 +            t.Fatalf("deriv %d has %d steps, want 1 atomic rule", i, len(
       d.Steps))
  148 +        }
  149 +        if err := physmath.Replay(f.Store, d, nil); err != nil {
  150 +            t.Fatalf("deriv %d replay failed: %v", i, err)
  151 +        }
  152 +    }
  153 +}
  154 +
  155 +// TestPartialsGoldens compares executed partials against hand-built tree
       s.
  156 +// Hand authority: raw DIFFERENTIATE-001/SIMPLIFY-001 normal forms observ
       ed
  157 +// from the engine (factored, no distributivity, zero-annihilation folded
       ).
  158 +func TestPartialsGoldens(t *testing.T) {
  159 +    tm, q := boundSyms()
  160 +    r0, r1, r2 := rat(0, 1), rat(1, 1), rat(2, 1)
  161 +    f := ms.Build()
  162 +    cases := []struct {
  163 +        name string
  164 +        ref  artifact.ArtifactRef
  165 +        want physmath.Expr
  166 +    }{
  167 +        {"DtRho1", f.DtRho1Ref, r0},
  168 +        {"DqJ1", f.DqJ1Ref, r0},
  169 +        {"Res1", f.Res1Ref, r0},
  170 +        {"J1", f.J1Ref, r2},
  171 +        {"V1", f.V1Ref, physmath.Mul(r2, physmath.Pow(r1, rat(-1, 1)))},
  172 +        {"DqS1", f.DqS1Ref, physmath.Add(physmath.Mul(q, r0), physmath.Mu
       l(r2, r1))},
  173 +        {"DqS2", f.DqS2Ref, physmath.Add(physmath.Mul(q, r0), physmath.Mu
       l(tm, r1))},
  174 +        {"DtR2", f.DtR2Ref, physmath.Add(r0,
  175 +            physmath.Mul(r0, r2, physmath.Pow(q, r2)),
  176 +            physmath.Mul(r1, r2, physmath.Pow(tm, r1)))},
  177 +        {"DqR2", f.DqR2Ref, physmath.Add(r0,
  178 +            physmath.Mul(r0, r2, physmath.Pow(tm, r2)),
  179 +            physmath.Mul(r1, r2, physmath.Pow(q, r1)))},
  180 +        {"D2qS2", f.D2qS2Ref, physmath.Add(
  181 +            physmath.Mul(q, r0), physmath.Mul(tm, r0),
  182 +            physmath.Mul(r0, r1), physmath.Mul(r0, r1))},
  183 +        {"Rho3", f.Rho3Ref, physmath.Mul(q, q)},
  184 +        {"J3", f.J3Ref, physmath.Mul(q, q)},
  185 +        {"V3", f.V3Ref, physmath.Mul(physmath.Mul(q, q),
  186 +            physmath.Pow(physmath.Mul(q, q), rat(-1, 1)))},
  187 +    }
  188 +    for _, c := range cases {
  189 +        assertCanonEq(t, mustResolve(t, f.Store, c.ref), c.want, c.name)
  190 +    }
  191 +}
  192 +
  193 +// TestCurrentAssemblyGoldens checks the composite current/density builds
       .
  194 +// Hand authority: R2 = t^2+q^2+1, so rho2 = R2^2 and j2 = rho2*d_qS2 wit
       h
  195 +// d_qS2 = t (raw Add(q*0, t*1)); v2 = j2*rho2^-1 by construction.
  196 +func TestCurrentAssemblyGoldens(t *testing.T) {
  197 +    tm, q := boundSyms()
  198 +    r1, r2 := rat(1, 1), rat(2, 1)
  199 +    r2body := physmath.Add(physmath.Pow(tm, r2), physmath.Pow(q, r2), r1)
  200 +    f := ms.Build()
  201 +    assertCanonEq(t, mustResolve(t, f.Store, f.Rho2Ref),
  202 +        physmath.Mul(r2body, r2body), "Rho2")
  203 +    assertCanonEq(t, mustResolve(t, f.Store, f.J2Ref),
  204 +        physmath.Mul(tm, r2body, r2body), "J2")
  205 +    assertCanonEq(t, mustResolve(t, f.Store, f.V2Ref),
  206 +        physmath.Mul(mustResolve(t, f.Store, f.J2Ref),
  207 +            physmath.Pow(mustResolve(t, f.Store, f.Rho2Ref), rat(-1, 1)))
       , "V2")
  208 +    assertCanonEq(t, mustResolve(t, f.Store, f.V4Ref),
  209 +        physmath.Mul(mustResolve(t, f.Store, f.J4Ref),
  210 +            physmath.Pow(mustResolve(t, f.Store, f.Rho4Ref), rat(-1, 1)))
       , "V4")
  211 +}

  213 +// TestSolutionResidualGolden decodes the EQ(Res1, 0) verdict relation.
  214 +func TestSolutionResidualGolden(t *testing.T) {
  215 +    f := ms.Build()
  216 +    _, payload, err := f.Store.Lookup(f.Res1ZeroRef)
  217 +    if err != nil {
  218 +        t.Fatal(err)
  219 +    }
  220 +    op, left, right := decodeRelationBody(t, payload)
  221 +    if op != string(physmath.OpEQ) {
  222 +        t.Fatalf("residual relation op = %q", op)
  223 +    }
  224 +    assertCanonEq(t, left, mustResolve(t, f.Store, f.Res1Ref), "Res1Zero
       left")
  225 +    assertCanonEq(t, right, rat(0, 1), "Res1Zero right")
  226 +}

  228 +// TestResidualDiscrimination pins the non-solution verdict by separation
       :
  229 +// Res2 is neither zero nor the solution residual, and both of its terms
  230 +// are nonzero. Hand authority: with R = t^2+q^2+1,
  231 +// d_t rho2 = 2R(2t) = 4tR, d_q j2 = d_q(tR^2) = t(2R)(2q) = 4tqR,
  232 +// so Res2 = 4tR(1+q), generically nonzero.
  233 +func TestResidualDiscrimination(t *testing.T) {
  234 +    f := ms.Build()
  235 +    res2 := mustResolve(t, f.Store, f.Res2Ref)
  236 +    assertCanonNe(t, res2, rat(0, 1), "Res2 vs zero")
  237 +    assertCanonNe(t, res2, mustResolve(t, f.Store, f.Res1Ref), "Res2 vs R
       es1")
  238 +    assertCanonNe(t, mustResolve(t, f.Store, f.DtRho2Ref), rat(0, 1), "Dt
       Rho2 vs zero")
  239 +    assertCanonNe(t, mustResolve(t, f.Store, f.DqJ2Ref), rat(0, 1), "DqJ2
        vs zero")
  240 +}

  242 +// TestProductRuleIdentity checks the executed divergence against a manua
       l
  243 +// product-rule assembly, both SIMPLIFY-staged. Hand authority:
  244 +// d_q j2 = d_q(rho2 * d_qS2) = (d_q rho2)(d_qS2) + rho2(d^2_q S2), with
  245 +// d_q rho2 = 2(d_qR2)R2, d_qS2 = t, d^2_qS2 = 0, so d_q j2 = 4tqR.
  246 +func TestProductRuleIdentity(t *testing.T) {
  247 +    f := ms.Build()
  248 +    _, payload, err := f.Store.Lookup(f.PRRelRef)
  249 +    if err != nil {
  250 +        t.Fatal(err)
  251 +    }
  252 +    op, left, right := decodeRelationBody(t, payload)
  253 +    if op != string(physmath.OpEQ) {
  254 +        t.Fatalf("product-rule relation op = %q", op)
  255 +    }
  256 +    assertCanonEq(t, left, right, "executed d_q j2 vs manual assembly")
  257 +}
  258 +
  259 +// sub11 substitutes (t,q) = (1,1) via EXPR-SUBSTITUTE-001. Each entry bi
       nds
  260 +// one (declaring artifact, pid) pair to the value 1.
  261 +func sub11(t *testing.T, s physmath.Store, target artifact.ArtifactRef, d
       ecls []artifact.ArtifactRef, pids []physmath.ParameterID) artifact.Artifa
       ctRef {
  262 +    t.Helper()
  263 +    one := putExpr(t, s, rat(1, 1))
  264 +    type entry struct {
  265 +        decl artifact.ArtifactRef
  266 +        pid  physmath.ParameterID
  267 +    }
  268 +    var es []entry
  269 +    for _, d := range decls {
  270 +        for _, p := range pids {
  271 +            es = append(es, entry{d, p})
  272 +        }
  273 +    }
  274 +    enc := make([][]byte, 0, len(es))
  275 +    sorted := append([]artifact.ArtifactRef(nil), decls...)
  276 +    sort.Slice(sorted, func(i, j int) bool {
  277 +        return string(sorted[i].Encode()) < string(sorted[j].Encode())
  278 +    })
  279 +    for _, e := range es {
  280 +        enc = append(enc, append(
  281 +            physmath.BoundParameterRef{DeclaringArtifactRef: e.decl, Para
       meterID: e.pid}.Encode(),
  282 +            one.Encode()...))
  283 +    }
  284 +    sort.Slice(enc, func(i, j int) bool { return string(enc[i]) < string(
       enc[j]) })
  285 +    params := artifact.EncodeUInt32(uint32(len(enc)))
  286 +    for _, b := range enc {
  287 +        params = append(params, b...)
  288 +    }
  289 +    out, err := physmath.Apply(s, physmath.Invocation{
  290 +        ProducerNamespace: physmath.NamespacePhysMath, RuleID: physmath.R
       uleExprSubstitute,
  291 +        InputRefs:  append([]artifact.ArtifactRef{target}, sorted...),
  292 +        Parameters: params,
  293 +    })
  294 +    if err != nil {
  295 +        t.Fatal(err)
  296 +    }
  297 +    return out.OutputRef
  298 +}
  299 +
  300 +// TestTravelingPair evaluates the pair-4 residual at (t,q) = (1,1).
  301 +// Hand authority: with u = q-3t and R = u^2+1, u(1,1) = -2, R = 5,
  302 +// d_t rho4 = 2R(2u)(-3) = -12Ru = 120,
  303 +// d_q j4 = d_q(3R^2) = 3(2R)(2u) = 12Ru = -120, residual 0.
  304 +// The machine contribution: substitution consistency, determinism, the
  305 +// nonzero/nontrivial pattern, and wrong-path discrimination. Only the
  306 +// final collapse 120 + (-120) = 0 rests on hand arithmetic.
  307 +func TestTravelingPair(t *testing.T) {
  308 +    f := ms.Build()
  309 +    pt := physmath.DeriveParameterID(0)
  310 +    pq := physmath.DeriveParameterID(1)
  311 +    sub := func(target artifact.ArtifactRef) artifact.ArtifactRef {
  312 +        t.Helper()
  313 +        return sub11(t, f.Store, target,
  314 +            []artifact.ArtifactRef{f.R4FnRef, f.S4FnRef}, []physmath.Para
       meterID{pt, pq})
  315 +    }
  316 +    sDt := mustResolve(t, f.Store, sub(f.DtRho4Ref))
  317 +    sDq := mustResolve(t, f.Store, sub(f.DqJ4Ref))
  318 +    sRes := mustResolve(t, f.Store, sub(f.Res4Ref))
  319 +    if r1, r2 := sub(f.Res4Ref), sub(f.Res4Ref); r1 != r2 {
  320 +        t.Fatal("substitution nondeterministic")
  321 +    }
  322 +    assertCanonNe(t, sDt, rat(0, 1), "substituted d_t rho4 vs zero")
  323 +    assertCanonNe(t, sDq, rat(0, 1), "substituted d_q j4 vs zero")
  324 +    assertCanonNe(t, sDt, sDq, "substituted terms vs each other")
  325 +    assertCanonNe(t, sRes, sDt, "substituted residual vs d_t term")
  326 +    assertCanonNe(t, sRes, sDq, "substituted residual vs d_q term")
  327 +    // Attempt: the substituted residual folds to 0 under SIMPLIFY-001.
  328 +    // Kept only if the engine's partial folder proves it here.
  329 +    simpRes := mustResolve(t, f.Store, applySimplify(t, f.Store, sub(f.Re
       s4Ref)))
  330 +    assertCanonEq(t, simpRes, rat(0, 1), "substituted Res4 folds to zero"
       )
  331 +}
  332 +
  333 +// TestAntiGoldens builds two wrong paths with the engine and separates
  334 +// each from the corrected residual and from zero. Hand authority:
  335 +// wrong-sign: ResW = 4tR - 4tqR = 4tR(1-q); dropped-factor:
  336 +// ResD = 4tR + d_qS2... with d_q(t) = 0, ResD = d_t rho2 = 4tR.
  337 +func TestAntiGoldens(t *testing.T) {
  338 +    f := ms.Build()
  339 +    pq := physmath.DeriveParameterID(1)
  340 +    res2 := mustResolve(t, f.Store, f.Res2Ref)
  341 +    // Wrong-sign current: jW = -j2, differentiated and summed by the eng
       ine.
  342 +    jW := putExpr(t, f.Store, physmath.Mul(rat(-1, 1), mustResolve(t, f.S
       tore, f.J2Ref)))
  343 +    dqJW, err := physmath.Apply(f.Store, physmath.Invocation{
  344 +        ProducerNamespace: physmath.NamespacePhysMath, RuleID: physmath.R
       uleDifferentiate,
  345 +        InputRefs:     []artifact.ArtifactRef{jW, f.R2FnRef},
  346 +        ConditionRefs: []artifact.ArtifactRef{f.Conds["torsionFree"]},
  347 +        Parameters:    diffParams(f.R2FnRef, pq, 1),
  348 +    })
  349 +    if err != nil {
  350 +        t.Fatal(err)
  351 +    }
  352 +    resW := mustResolve(t, f.Store, applySimplify(t, f.Store,
  353 +        putExpr(t, f.Store, physmath.Add(mustResolve(t, f.Store, f.DtRho2
       Ref),
  354 +            mustResolve(t, f.Store, dqJW.OutputRef)))))
  355 +    assertCanonNe(t, resW, res2, "wrong-sign residual vs Res2")
  356 +    assertCanonNe(t, resW, rat(0, 1), "wrong-sign residual vs zero")
  357 +    // Dropped-factor divergence: d_t rho2 + d_qS2 (missing the j2 assemb
       ly).
  358 +    resD := mustResolve(t, f.Store, applySimplify(t, f.Store,
  359 +        putExpr(t, f.Store, physmath.Add(mustResolve(t, f.Store, f.DtRho2
       Ref),
  360 +            mustResolve(t, f.Store, f.DqS2Ref)))))
  361 +    assertCanonNe(t, resD, res2, "dropped-factor residual vs Res2")
  362 +    assertCanonNe(t, resD, rat(0, 1), "dropped-factor residual vs zero")
  363 +    assertCanonNe(t, resW, resD, "wrong-sign vs dropped-factor")
  364 +}

  376 +// TestRepresentedOnly scans every derivation's full reference footprint
  377 +// and requires the three claim relations to be absent from all of them.
  378 +func TestRepresentedOnly(t *testing.T) {
  379 +    f := ms.Build()
  380 +    banned := map[artifact.ArtifactRef]string{
  381 +        f.FlowRelRef: "FlowRel", f.PushRelRef: "PushRel", f.EquivRelRef:
       "EquivRel",
  382 +    }
  383 +    for i, dref := range f.Derivs {
  384 +        _, payload, err := f.Store.Lookup(dref)
  385 +        if err != nil {
  386 +            t.Fatal(err)
  387 +        }
  388 +        d, err := physmath.DecodeDerivation(payload)
  389 +        if err != nil {
  390 +            t.Fatal(err)
  391 +        }
  392 +        var refs []artifact.ArtifactRef
  393 +        refs = append(refs, d.PremiseRefs...)
  394 +        refs = append(refs, d.ResultRefs...)
  395 +        for _, st := range d.Steps {
  396 +            refs = append(refs, st.InputRefs...)
  397 +            refs = append(refs, st.OutputRef)
  398 +            refs = append(refs, st.ConditionRefs...)
  399 +        }
  400 +        for _, r := range refs {
  401 +            if name, ok := banned[r]; ok {
  402 +                t.Fatalf("deriv %d references represented-only %s", i, na
       me)
  403 +            }
  404 +        }
  405 +    }
  406 +}

  408 +// TestLimitationRecord pins the rule allowlist: only SIMPLIFY-001 and
  409 +// DIFFERENTIATE-001 appear in any derivation step.
  410 +func TestLimitationRecord(t *testing.T) {
  411 +    f := ms.Build()
  412 +    for i, dref := range f.Derivs {
  413 +        _, payload, err := f.Store.Lookup(dref)
  414 +        if err != nil {
  415 +            t.Fatal(err)
  416 +        }
  417 +        d, err := physmath.DecodeDerivation(payload)
  418 +        if err != nil {
  419 +            t.Fatal(err)
  420 +        }
  421 +        for _, st := range d.Steps {
  422 +            if st.ProducerNamespace != physmath.NamespacePhysMath {
  423 +                t.Fatalf("deriv %d step uses namespace %q", i, st.Produce
       rNamespace)
  424 +            }
  425 +            if st.RuleID != physmath.RuleExprSimplify && st.RuleID != phy
       smath.RuleDifferentiate {
  426 +                t.Fatalf("deriv %d step uses rule %q", i, st.RuleID)
  427 +            }
  428 +        }
  429 +    }
  430 +}

  473 +// TestRebuildDeterminism builds the fixture twice and byte-compares ever
       y
  474 +// exported payload: the artifact is a pure function of the code.
  475 +func TestRebuildDeterminism(t *testing.T) {
  476 +    a := ms.Build()
  477 +    b := ms.Build()
  478 +    refs := func(f *ms.Fixture) []artifact.ArtifactRef {
  479 +        out := []artifact.ArtifactRef{f.ChartRef,
  480 +            f.R1FnRef, f.R1Ref, f.S1FnRef, f.S1Ref, f.Rho1Ref, f.DtRho1Re
       f,
  481 +            f.DqS1Ref, f.J1Ref, f.DqJ1Ref, f.Res1Ref, f.Res1ZeroRef,
  482 +            f.V1Ref, f.Guide1RelRef,
  483 +            f.R2FnRef, f.R2Ref, f.S2FnRef, f.S2Ref, f.Rho2Ref, f.DtRho2Re
       f,
  484 +            f.DqS2Ref, f.DtR2Ref, f.DqR2Ref, f.D2qS2Ref, f.J2Ref, f.DqJ2R
       ef,
  485 +            f.DqJ2SRef, f.Res2Ref, f.PRRelRef, f.V2Ref, f.Guide2RelRef,
  486 +            f.R3FnRef, f.R3Ref, f.S3FnRef, f.S3Ref, f.Rho3Ref, f.J3Ref,
  487 +            f.V3Ref, f.Guide3RelRef,
  488 +            f.R4FnRef, f.R4Ref, f.S4FnRef, f.S4Ref, f.Rho4Ref, f.DtRho4Re
       f,
  489 +            f.DqS4Ref, f.J4Ref, f.DqJ4Ref, f.Res4Ref, f.V4Ref, f.Guide4Re
       lRef,
  490 +            f.FlowRelRef, f.PushRelRef, f.EquivRelRef}
  491 +        out = append(out, f.Derivs...)
  492 +        for _, k := range []string{"torsionFree", "rhoNonzero", "rho2Nonz
       ero", "rho3Nonzero", "rho4Nonzero"} {
  493 +            out = append(out, f.Conds[k])
  494 +        }
  495 +        for _, k := range []string{"t", "q", "rhoT", "flowT", "pushT"} {
  496 +            out = append(out, f.Symbols[k])
  497 +        }
  498 +        return out
  499 +    }
  500 +    ra, rb := refs(a), refs(b)
  501 +    if len(ra) != len(rb) {
  502 +        t.Fatal("ref list length mismatch")
  503 +    }
  504 +    for i := range ra {
  505 +        fa, pa, err := a.Store.Lookup(ra[i])
  506 +        if err != nil {
  507 +            t.Fatal(err)
  508 +        }
  509 +        fb, pb, err := b.Store.Lookup(rb[i])
  510 +        if err != nil {
  511 +            t.Fatal(err)
  512 +        }
  513 +        if fa != fb || !bytes.Equal(pa, pb) {
  514 +            t.Fatalf("ref %d differs across rebuilds", i)
  515 +        }
  516 +    }
  517 +}

  25 +func TestDebugForms(t *testing.T) {

  13 +func dhex(t *testing.T, e physmath.Expr) string {
  14 +    t.Helper()
  15 +    c, err := physmath.Canonicalize(e)
  16 +    if err != nil {
  17 +        t.Fatal(err)
  18 +    }
  19 +    b, err := c.Encode()
  20 +    if err != nil {
  21 +        t.Fatal(err)
  22 +    }
  23 +    return hex.EncodeToString(b)
  24 +}
  25 +
  26 +func dsub(t *testing.T, s physmath.Store, target, decl artifact.ArtifactRe
      f, pt, pq physmath.ParameterID) artifact.ArtifactRef {
  27 +    t.Helper()
  28 +    one := putExpr(t, s, rat(1, 1))
  29 +    mk := func(pid physmath.ParameterID) []byte {
  30 +        return append(physmath.BoundParameterRef{DeclaringArtifactRef: dec
      l, ParameterID: pid}.Encode(), one.Encode()...)
  31 +    }
  32 +    e1, e2 := mk(pt), mk(pq)
  33 +    if string(e1) > string(e2) {
  34 +        e1, e2 = e2, e1
  35 +    }
  36 +    params := append(artifact.EncodeUInt32(2), append(e1, e2...)...)
  37 +    out, err := physmath.Apply(s, physmath.Invocation{
  38 +        ProducerNamespace: physmath.NamespacePhysMath, RuleID: physmath.Ru
      leExprSubstitute,
  39 +        InputRefs:  []artifact.ArtifactRef{target, decl},
  40 +        Parameters: params,
  41 +    })
  42 +    if err != nil {
  43 +        t.Fatal(err)
  44 +    }
  45 +    return out.OutputRef
  46 +}
  47 +
  48 +func TestDebugForms(t *testing.T) {
  49 +    tm, q := boundSyms()
  50 +    r0, r1, r2 := rat(0, 1), rat(1, 1), rat(2, 1)

      ve(t, f.Store, ref) }

│     params := append(artifact.EncodeUInt32(2), append(e1, e2...)...)
│     out, err := physmath.Apply(s, physmath.Invocation{
│         ProducerNamespace: physmath.NamespacePhysMath, RuleID:
│ physmath.RuleExprSubstitute,
│         InputRefs:  []artifact.ArtifactRef{target, decl},
│         Parameters: params,
│     })
│     if err != nil {
│         t.Fatal(err)
│     }
│     return out.OutputRef
│ }
│
│ '''
│ s = s.replace(old_sub, new_sub)
│
│ # 3. Traveling test: new sub closure; cut the failed folding attempt.
│ rep('''    sub := func(target artifact.ArtifactRef) artifact.ArtifactRef {
│         t.Helper()
│         return sub11(t, f.Store, target,
│             []artifact.ArtifactRef{f.R4FnRef, f.S4FnRef},

│ sub(f.Res4Ref)))
│     assertCanonEq(t, simpRes, rat(0, 1), "substituted Res4 folds to zero")''',

│     // SIMPLIFY-001 leaves substituted trees unfolded (Pow(1,n) remnants,
│     // no rational-tree arithmetic), so the final 120 + (-120) = 0 step
│     // rests on the hand algebra above — the same boundary as the
│     // minisuperspace substitution precedent. No assertion is made here
│     // that the engine cannot prove.''')

│ # 6. Product-rule test restructure: linkage + totality + hand proof.
│ start = s.index('// TestProductRuleIdentity checks the executed divergence')
│ end = s.index('// sub11 substitutes')
│ new_pr = '''// TestProductRuleIdentity pins the product-rule relation as
│ linkage plus
│ // hand-authorized equation. PROTOCOL NOTE (negative result, kept honest):
│ // the simp-staged executed and manual forms are DIFFERENT factored normal
│ // forms (Mul(t,...) vs Mul(q,t,...)); SIMPLIFY-001 has no distributivity,
│ // like-term collection, or const-folding, so the engine cannot reduce them
│ // to a common form — not even after (t,q)=(1,1) substitution. The machine
│ // contribution: (1) the relation links the stored executed divergence
│ // (left bytes == DqJ2S payload); (2) both sides substitute totally at
│ // (1,1) — idempotent re-substitution proves no symbols remain — and stay
│ // nonzero. The equation itself (both sides denote d_q j2) is hand-proved.
│ // Hand authority: executed is Diff(Mul(t,R2,R2)) = (d_q t)R2^2 +
│ // t(2R2)(d_qR2) = 0 + t(2R2)(2q) = 4tqR2; manual is
│ // 2R2(d_qR2)(d_qS2) + rho2(d^2_qS2) = 2R2(2q)t + R2^2(0) = 4tqR2.
│ func TestProductRuleIdentity(t *testing.T) {
│     f := ms.Build()
│     _, payload, err := f.Store.Lookup(f.PRRelRef)
│     if err != nil {
│         t.Fatal(err)
│     }
│     op, left, right := decodeRelationBody(t, payload)
│     if op != string(physmath.OpEQ) {
│         t.Fatalf("product-rule relation op = %q", op)
│     }
│     _, dqJ2S, err := f.Store.Lookup(f.DqJ2SRef)
│     if err != nil {
│         t.Fatal(err)
│     }
│     lb, err := left.Encode()
│     if err != nil {
│         t.Fatal(err)
│     }
│     if !bytes.Equal(lb, dqJ2S) {
│         t.Fatal("product-rule left is not the stored executed divergence")
│     }
│     pt := physmath.DeriveParameterID(0)
│     pq := physmath.DeriveParameterID(1)
│     sub := func(target artifact.ArtifactRef) artifact.ArtifactRef {
│         t.Helper()
│         return sub11(t, f.Store, target, f.R2FnRef, pt, pq)
│     }
│     sL := sub(putExpr(t, f.Store, left))
│     sR := sub(putExpr(t, f.Store, right))
│     if again := sub(putExpr(t, f.Store, left)); again != sL {
│         t.Fatal("left substitution nondeterministic")
│     }
│     if closed := sub(sL); closed != sL {
│         t.Fatal("left substitution not total")
│     }
│     if closed := sub(sR); closed != sR {
│         t.Fatal("right substitution not total")
│     }
│     assertCanonNe(t, mustResolve(t, f.Store, sL), rat(0, 1), "substituted
│ executed divergence vs zero")
│     assertCanonNe(t, mustResolve(t, f.Store, sR), rat(0, 1), "substituted
│ manual assembly vs zero")
│ }
│
│ '''
│ s = s[:start] + new_pr + s[end:]
│ open(p, 'w').write(s)
│ print('derivation_test patched')

   1 +# AGENTS.md — theorems/bohm-equiv
   2 +
   3 +Bohmian equivariance (Benchmark 2) evidence fixture. Audit:
   4 +`../../audit/` (docs 01–03; S1 freeze, B-register, continuity DERIVED,
   5 +local equivariance DERIVED, global SOURCE-REPORTED via TT). This area
   6 +is representation + regression only: it executes fragments of the
   7 +audit chain on explicit 1D test inputs and replays the evidence. It
   8 +proves nothing about the general R^{3N} theory.
   9 +
  10 +PLACEMENT NOTE: plan7.1 prescribed `phys-lib/bohm/equivariance/`; a
  11 +direct user order ("do not modify any phys codebase") relocated the
  12 +artifact here as a standalone module on the qsg-t001 pattern. The
  13 +shared repos are read-only inputs, never targets.
  14 +
  15 +## Layout
  16 +
  17 +- `go.mod`, `go.work` — new module `github.com/PithomLabs/bohm-equiv`;
  18 +  colocated workspace over phys-artifact + phys-math (absolute
  19 +  `use` paths: this module lives outside `/home/chaschel/Documents/go`).
  20 +  No phys-lib import: the fixture uses only the representation layer.
  21 +- `fixture.go` — `Build` (four 1D test pairs, 30 one-step
  22 +  DIFFERENTIATE/SIMPLIFY derivations) and `(*Fixture).Verify`
  23 +  (replays + linkage gates). Gates compare resolved payload bytes
  24 +  or canonical forms, never bare refs.
  25 +- `manifest.json` — premises/assumptions/limitations manifest.
  26 +- `source.json`, `source_mapping.json` — TT import identity +
  27 +  structural equation-to-fixture linkage (linkage only, never
  28 +  semantic equivalence; enforced by test).
  29 +- `protocol/derivation_test.go` — executable suite (15 tests).
  30 +
  31 +## Commands (GOCACHE is read-only; always override)
  32 +
  33 +```
  34 +export GOWORK=$PWD/go.work GOCACHE=/tmp/bohm-equiv-gocache GOPROXY=off
  35 +go build ./...
  36 +go test -count=1 ./...
  37 +go vet ./...
  38 +gofmt -l .   # must print nothing
  39 +```
  40 +
  41 +## What replay proves (and does not)
  42 +
  43 +Proves: 30 single-step derivations replay; executed partials match
  44 +hand-built raw engine forms (power rule decrements: d_t(q^2) is raw
  45 +Mul(0,2,q^1)); solution-pair residual is engine-zero; non-solution
  46 +residual separates from zero and from the solution residual;
  47 +product-rule relation links the stored executed divergence and both
  48 +sides substitute totally at (t,q)=(1,1); traveling-pair substitution
  49 +is consistent, deterministic, and nontrivial; two engine-built wrong
  50 +paths separate from the corrected residual and from zero; guidance
  51 +verdicts require their NEQ(rho,0) conditions; flow/pushforward/
  52 +equivariance relations appear in zero derivation footprints; only
  53 +SIMPLIFY-001 and DIFFERENTIATE-001 appear; rebuilds are byte-identical.
  54 +
  55 +Does NOT prove: the product-rule EQ agreement or the pair-4
  56 +cancellation — both rest on adjacent hand algebra because
  57 +SIMPLIFY-001 cannot decide rational-tree equality (no
  58 +distributivity, like-term collection, or const-folding; recorded as
  59 +limitation `equality_not_decided`, demonstrated by the
  60 +Mul(t,...)-vs-Mul(q,t,...) normal-form split and the unfolded
  61 +substituted trees). Does not prove flow existence, the transport
  62 +identity, uniqueness, node behavior, (E-loc), or (E-glob) — those
  63 +live in the audit record only, as derived, sourced, or excluded.
  64 +
  65 +## Golden policy
  66 +
  67 +Every golden cites the hand derivation that produced it (adjacent
  68 +protocol comments): raw engine normal forms, pair algebras
  69 +(R2: 4tR(1+q); R4: -12Ru/+12Ru at u=q-3t), and the audit sections
  70 +(02:C, 02:j-def, 03:guide, 03:T, 03:E-loc, 03:flow, 01:S1).
  71 +Changing a golden requires changing the cited audit section first.
  72 +
  73 +## No-go rules
  74 +
  75 +- No imports beyond phys-artifact + phys-math.
  76 +- No writes outside this directory and `../../audit/`: shared repos
  77 +  are read-only; the committed `/home/chaschel/Documents/go/go.work`
  78 +  is untouched; nothing is committed anywhere.
  79 +- No test may assert what the engine cannot prove: a failed machine
  80 +  attempt becomes a recorded negative result, never a weakened gate.
  81 +- No weakening a failing gate: fix the fixture/test or record why
  82 +  the expectation is wrong in the manifest limitations.
  83 +- No relativistic/QFT/typicality/measurement content (doc 01 §4).

  The five platform questions

  1. Reused from QSG-T001: the household, not the math — colocated-go.work
     standalone pattern, Build/Verify + derivation replay, manifest/sidecar
     conventions, golden/anti-golden/tamper/condition-binding test shapes,
     canonical-compare discipline. Representation layer reused:
     expression/relation/condition/derivation families, SIMPLIFY-001,
     SUBSTITUTE-001.
  2. Genuinely new: executed DIFFERENTIATE-001 chains (QSG executed none),
     including nested second derivatives; density/current/quotient assembly on
     test inputs; point-substitution verdicts with idempotence-proved totality;
     represented-only claim relations with footprint-scan enforcement.
  3. PhysMath unpolluted: yes — zero PhysMath changes, zero shared-repo writes
     of any kind. Imports are phys-artifact + phys-math only; all Bohmian
     semantics live in TOE-v6 docs, names, and comments.
  4. Stays theory-specific:
     wavefunction/density/current/guidance/flow/equivariance semantics,
     test-pair definitions, condition vocabularies, claim-relation linkage — and
     anything measure/ODE/PDE if ever built (belongs in theory or analysis
     libraries, not the core).
  5. Demonstrated deficiencies (prioritized): (i) no decision procedure for
     rational-tree equality — the #1 finding: SIMPLIFY-001 is non-confluent
     (executed Mul(t,…) vs manual Mul(q,t,…) normal forms), with no
     distributivity, like-term collection, or const-folding; even substituted
     trees don't fold (simp(sub(Res4))≠0, both recorded as negative results,
     limitation equality_not_decided); (ii) no complex arithmetic — forced the
     real (R,S) test-units formulation, Schrödinger residual unrepresentable;
     (iii) no integration/ODE/measure/pushforward execution — flow, transport,
     uniqueness, (E-loc)/(E-glob) represented-only; (iv) raw-form-only
     differentiation needing hand-staged partials; (v) substitution evaluates
     but can't fold; (vi) no node/domain semantics beyond NEQ conditions.

  QSG-vs-Bohm comparison

  QSG-T001 pins curved-spacetime c-number goldens with SIMPLIFY-only execution
  and a sketch for the proof remainder; Bohmian equivariance executes
  differential chains (DIF/SIMPLIFY/SUBSTITUTE) on probability-flow fragments
  with represented-only relations for the theorem remainder. Both confirm the
  same joint result: the platform reliably pins and replays explicit
  computations with honest supported-subset tests, but cannot close equational
  proofs (gap i) or represent analysis content (gaps ii–iii). Per safeguard S2,
  this is an initial cross-theory coverage assessment, not a universality claim.

  Goal

  the local-equivariance conclusion in the Bohm audit record
  (trajectory-identity route, conditional pushforward), strengthen B2's

  changes to the executable artifact, which the review says to preserve.

  Success Criteria

  • Doc 01 B2 explicitly requires enough regularity for the full spatial Hessian
    of ψ (not just continuous ∇ψ, Δψ), stated as the Lipschitz foundation for j
    and v.
  • Doc 03 derives the trajectory-level density-transport identity
    ρ_t(Φ(q₀))|det DΦ(q₀)| = ρ_{t₀}(q₀) on trajectories staying in {ρ>0}, then
    states the (E-loc) pushforward theorem as CONDITIONAL on two new explicit
    premises: a.e. flow existence on a common interval (B8) and no probability
    loss through nodal/escape boundaries (B9).
  • Doc 03 §3 uniqueness lemma stays DERIVED under U1, but carries an explicit
    flag that U1/B4 decay (spatial infinity) does not control flux through
    internal nodal boundaries — which is why the §4 proof no longer routes
    through PDE uniqueness on the punctured domain.
  • Doc 03 §5 keeps a visible, explicit sentence that the TT global import
    applies only to the TT potential/wavefunction class (operator-domain,
    normalization conditions) and does not auto-generalize to every classical S1
    solution.
  • All disposition statements are mutually consistent (doc 03 title, §4 header,
    §6; doc 01 §2 pointer); the fixture, tests, sidecars, and plan7.1_imp.md are
    byte-untouched; both gates still green.

   92 +  exists for all \(t\in[t_0,t_1]\) for \(\mu_{t_0}\)-almost every
